DECLARE @problems int = 0, @done int = 0, @msg nvarchar(2000);

SELECT @done = COUNT(*) FROM @expected e
WHERE OBJECT_DEFINITION(OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.' + e.name)) LIKE N'%QPID_OPT V4-10%';
IF @done = 49
BEGIN
    PRINT 'ALREADY DEPLOYED: V4-10 is live on all 49 procedures. Nothing changed.';
    SET NOEXEC ON;
END
ELSE
BEGIN
    IF @done > 0
    BEGIN
        RAISERROR('REFUSED: V4-10 is live on some procedures only. Do not continue; investigate.', 16, 1);
        SET @problems += 1;
    END

    -- 1. Every procedure must be the version these edits were built on.
    DECLARE @n sysname, @len int, @mark nvarchar(40), @def nvarchar(max);
    DECLARE c CURSOR LOCAL FAST_FORWARD FOR SELECT name, live_len, other_mark FROM @expected;
    OPEN c;
    FETCH NEXT FROM c INTO @n, @len, @mark;
    WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
        SET @def = OBJECT_DEFINITION(OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.' + @n));
        IF @def IS NULL OR (LEN(@def) <> @len AND (@mark IS NULL OR @def NOT LIKE N'%' + @mark + N'%'))
        BEGIN
            SET @msg = N'REFUSED: dbo.' + @n + N' live LEN ' + ISNULL(CONVERT(nvarchar(20), LEN(@def)), N'(missing)')
                     + N', expected ' + CONVERT(nvarchar(20), @len) + N'.';
            RAISERROR(@msg, 16, 1);
            SET @problems += 1;
        END
        FETCH NEXT FROM c INTO @n, @len, @mark;
    END
    CLOSE c;
    DEALLOCATE c;

    -- 2. No writer may exist that this script does not know about.
    ;WITH d AS (
        SELECT o.name,
               REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(LOWER(m.definition),
                   CHAR(13), N' '), CHAR(10), N' '), CHAR(9), N' '), N'[', N''), N']', N''),
                   N'         ', N' '), N'     ', N' '), N'   ', N' '), N'  ', N' ') AS n
        FROM sys.sql_modules m
        JOIN sys.objects o ON o.object_id = m.object_id
    )
    SELECT @msg = STUFF((SELECT N', ' + d.name FROM d
        WHERE d.name NOT IN (SELECT name FROM @known)
        AND (d.n LIKE N'%insert into bt_customer_master%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into dbo.bt_customer_master%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert bt_customer_master%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert dbo.bt_customer_master%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into bt_address_master%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into dbo.bt_address_master%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert bt_address_master%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert dbo.bt_address_master%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into bt_contacts_master%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into dbo.bt_contacts_master%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert bt_contacts_master%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert dbo.bt_contacts_master%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into bt_enquiry_master%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into dbo.bt_enquiry_master%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert bt_enquiry_master%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert dbo.bt_enquiry_master%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into bt_act_hist%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into dbo.bt_act_hist%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert bt_act_hist%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert dbo.bt_act_hist%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into bt_act_standards%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into dbo.bt_act_standards%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert bt_act_standards%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert dbo.bt_act_standards%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into bt_quote_master%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into dbo.bt_quote_master%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert bt_quote_master%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert dbo.bt_quote_master%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into bt_estimate_master%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into dbo.bt_estimate_master%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert bt_estimate_master%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert dbo.bt_estimate_master%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into cdms_act_ae_extra%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into dbo.cdms_act_ae_extra%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert cdms_act_ae_extra%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert dbo.cdms_act_ae_extra%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into bt_act_billofmaterials%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into dbo.bt_act_billofmaterials%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert bt_act_billofmaterials%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert dbo.bt_act_billofmaterials%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into bt_act_taskcategory%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert into dbo.bt_act_taskcategory%'
        OR d.n LIKE N'%insert bt_act_taskcategory%' OR d.n LIKE N'%insert dbo.bt_act_taskcategory%')
        FOR XML PATH('')), 1, 2, N'');
    IF @msg IS NOT NULL
    BEGIN
        SET @msg = N'REFUSED: unknown objects insert into these tables: ' + LEFT(@msg, 1800);
        RAISERROR(@msg, 16, 1);
        SET @problems += 1;
    END

    -- 3. Nobody may be writing to the tables now: an old call holding a MAX()+1 value would clash.
    SET @msg = NULL;
    SELECT @msg = STUFF((SELECT DISTINCT N', ' + CONVERT(nvarchar(10), l.request_session_id)
        FROM sys.dm_tran_locks l
        WHERE l.resource_database_id = DB_ID()
          AND l.resource_type = 'OBJECT'
          AND l.request_mode IN ('IX', 'X', 'SIX')
          AND l.resource_associated_entity_id IN (OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER'), OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.BT_ADDRESS_MASTER'), OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.BT_CONTACTS_MASTER'), OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER'), OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.BT_ACT_HIST'), OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.BT_ACT_STANDARDS'), OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.BT_QUOTE_MASTER'), OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.BT_ESTIMATE_MASTER'), OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.CDMS_ACT_AE_EXTRA'), OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS'), OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY'))
          AND l.request_session_id <> @@SPID
        FOR XML PATH('')), 1, 2, N'');
    IF @msg IS NOT NULL
    BEGIN
        SET @msg = N'REFUSED: these sessions are writing to the tables: ' + @msg + N'. Stop the writers and run again.';
        RAISERROR(@msg, 16, 1);
        SET @problems += 1;
    END

    -- 3b. An id column with its own identity or default would mean the source DDL is out of date.
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM (VALUES (N'dbo.BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER', N'CUST_ID'), (N'dbo.BT_ADDRESS_MASTER', N'ADDRESS_ID'), (N'dbo.BT_CONTACTS_MASTER', N'con_contactID'), (N'dbo.BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER', N'AE_AEID'), (N'dbo.BT_ACT_HIST', N'HIST_HISTID'), (N'dbo.BT_ACT_STANDARDS', N'STA_ID'), (N'dbo.BT_QUOTE_MASTER', N'AE_AEID'), (N'dbo.BT_ESTIMATE_MASTER', N'AE_AEID'), (N'dbo.CDMS_ACT_AE_EXTRA', N'EX_ID'), (N'dbo.BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS', N'BOM_bomID'), (N'dbo.BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY', N'TC_TCID')) v(t, c)
               JOIN sys.columns sc ON sc.object_id = OBJECT_ID(v.t) AND sc.name = v.c
               WHERE sc.is_identity = 1 OR sc.default_object_id <> 0)
    BEGIN
        RAISERROR('REFUSED: an id column has an identity or default. Run V4-10.1 f and send the result.', 16, 1);
        SET @problems += 1;
    END

    -- 4. The sequences must not exist yet.
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.sequences WHERE name IN (N'SEQ_BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER', N'SEQ_BT_ADDRESS_MASTER', N'SEQ_BT_CONTACTS_MASTER', N'SEQ_BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER', N'SEQ_BT_ACT_HIST', N'SEQ_BT_ACT_STANDARDS', N'SEQ_BT_QUOTE_MASTER', N'SEQ_BT_ESTIMATE_MASTER', N'SEQ_CDMS_ACT_AE_EXTRA', N'SEQ_BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS', N'SEQ_BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY'))
    BEGIN
        RAISERROR('REFUSED: a V4-10 sequence already exists, but the procedures are not changed.', 16, 1);
        SET @problems += 1;
    END

    IF @problems > 0
    BEGIN
        RAISERROR('NOT DEPLOYED: nothing changed. Fix the REFUSED items above.', 16, 1);
        SET NOEXEC ON;
    END
END
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_CREATE_NEW_CUSTOMER] 
	@ploggedonUser				INT,
	@pCustType					VARCHAR(15),
	@pCustOrgName 				VARCHAR(50),
	@pTitle 				VARCHAR(50),
	@pFirstname 				VARCHAR(50),
	@pLastname 				VARCHAR(50),
	@pTelephone 				VARCHAR(50)= NULL,
	@pCFSID 					VARCHAR(50)= NULL,
	@pMobile 					VARCHAR(50)= NULL,
	@pEmail						VARCHAR(50)= NULL,
	@pCustRelationshipManager	int = NULL,
	@pCustref					varchar(50) OUTPUT,
	@pErrorMessage				VARCHAR(max) OUTPUT 
	
AS
BEGIN

	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	DECLARE @vCustId int;
	DECLARE @vConId int;
	Declare @NewCustRef Varchar(50)
	
BEGIN TRY
		SET @pErrorMessage = ''
			
	     SET @vCustId = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/
		 Set @NewCustRef='CUST'+CONVERT(varchar(max),@vCustId)	
		 SET @vConId = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_CONTACTS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/
		IF @pCustType=	'Commercial'
		BEGIN	
			
		INSERT INTO BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER
				   (CUST_ID,
				    CUST_REFERENCE,
					CUST_ORG_Name,
					CUST_TELEPHONE,
					CFSID,
					CREATED_ON,
					CREATED_BY,
					UPDATED_ON,
					UPDATED_BY,
					CUST_TYPE,
					CUST_DELETED,SOURCE_SYS,custRelationshipManager)
			 SELECT
					@vCustId,
					@NewCustRef,
					@pCustOrgName,
					@pTelephone,
					@pCFSID,
					GETDATE(),
					@ploggedonUser,
					GETDATE(),
					@ploggedonUser,
					'Commercial','N','QPID',@pCustRelationshipManager;

		INSERT INTO BT_CONTACTS_MASTER
				   ([con_contactID],[con_parentID], [con_parentType],[con_surname] ,[con_forename]
				   ,[con_personalTitle],[con_jobTitle] ,[con_telephone]
				   ,[con_email] ,[con_mobile] ,[con_dateCreated],[con_createdBy],[con_lastUpdated],
				   [con_lastUpdatedBy],[con_deleted],[con_CFSID]
				   )
					 SELECT
					@vConId,@vCustId,1,'',@pCustOrgName,'NA','',@pTelephone
					,@pEmail,@pMobile,GETDATE(),
					@ploggedonUser,GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,'Y',@pCFSID
		END
		IF @pCustType ='ICP'
		BEGIN	
			
		INSERT INTO BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER
				   (CUST_ID,
				    CUST_REFERENCE,
					CUST_ORG_Name,
					CUST_TELEPHONE,
					CFSID,
					CREATED_ON,
					CREATED_BY,
					UPDATED_ON,
					UPDATED_BY,
					CUST_TYPE,
					CUST_DELETED,SOURCE_SYS,custRelationshipManager)
			 SELECT
					@vCustId,
					@NewCustRef,
					@pCustOrgName,
					@pTelephone,
					@pCFSID,
					GETDATE(),
					@ploggedonUser,
					GETDATE(),
					@ploggedonUser,
					'ICP','N','QPID',@pCustRelationshipManager;

		INSERT INTO BT_CONTACTS_MASTER
				   ([con_contactID],[con_parentID], [con_parentType],[con_surname] ,[con_forename]
				   ,[con_personalTitle],[con_jobTitle] ,[con_telephone]
				   ,[con_email] ,[con_mobile] ,[con_dateCreated],[con_createdBy],[con_lastUpdated],
				   [con_lastUpdatedBy],[con_deleted],[con_CFSID]
				   )
					 SELECT
					@vConId,@vCustId,1,'',@pCustOrgName,'NA','',@pTelephone
					,@pEmail,@pMobile,GETDATE(),
					@ploggedonUser,GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,'Y',@pCFSID
		END
		IF @pCustType ='Domestic'
		BEGIN
				INSERT INTO BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER
				   (CUST_ID,
				    CUST_REFERENCE,
					CUST_ORG_Name,
					CUST_TELEPHONE,
					CFSID,
					CREATED_ON,
					CREATED_BY,
					UPDATED_ON,
					UPDATED_BY,
					CUST_TYPE,CUST_DELETED,SOURCE_SYS,custRelationshipManager)
			 SELECT
					@vCustId,
					@NewCustRef,
					@pTitle+' '+@pFirstname +' '+@pLastname,
					@pTelephone,
					@pCFSID,
					GETDATE(),
					@ploggedonUser,
					GETDATE(),
					@ploggedonUser,
					'Domestic','N','QPID',@pCustRelationshipManager;
				
					INSERT INTO BT_CONTACTS_MASTER
				   ([con_contactID],[con_parentID], [con_parentType],[con_surname] ,[con_forename]
				   ,[con_personalTitle],[con_jobTitle] ,[con_telephone],
				    [con_email] ,[con_mobile] ,[con_dateCreated],[con_createdBy],[con_lastUpdated],
				   [con_lastUpdatedBy],[con_deleted],[con_CFSID],con_contactName
				   )
					 SELECT
					@vConId,@vCustId,1,@pLastname,@pFirstname,@pTitle,'',@pTelephone,@pEmail,@pMobile,GETDATE(),
					@ploggedonUser,GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,'N',@pCFSID,@pTitle+' '+@pFirstname+' '+@pLastname
		END

		SELECT @pCustref=@NewCustRef;
		print @pCustref
	--	return @pCustref 
		
END TRY

BEGIN CATCH
		SELECT @pErrorMessage='Error Code: ' + CAST (ERROR_NUMBER() AS VARCHAR(10)) + ', Error: ' + ERROR_MESSAGE()
		RETURN 1
END CATCH	
END 




GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

-- ---- uspInsertNewCustomerDetails (2 sites)
IF @@TRANCOUNT = 0 SET NOEXEC ON;
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[uspInsertNewCustomerDetails]
	@ploggedonUser				VARCHAR(25),
	@pCustType					VARCHAR(15),
	@pCustOrgName 				VARCHAR(50),
	@pTelephone 				VARCHAR(50)= NULL,
	@pCFSID 					VARCHAR(50)= NULL,
	--@pcmbElecSupplier			INT = NULL,
	--@pcompanyID 				INT=1,
	--@pCustUtility 				VARCHAR(1)= NULL,
	--@pFax 						VARCHAR(50)= NULL,
	@pMobile 					VARCHAR(50)= NULL,
	--@pContCFSID 				INT= NULL,
	@pEmail						VARCHAR(50)= NULL,
	--@pCustId					INT OUTPUT,
	@pCustref					varchar(25) OUTPUT,
	@pErrorMessage				VARCHAR(5000) OUTPUT 

/**************************************************************											
--	Procedure Name	:	uspInsertNewCustomerDetails
--	Description		:	Insert Customer Details
--	Parameters		:	
						
						@pErrorMessage - Output parameter to return the error message

--	Return Value	:	0	Success			
--						1	Failure

--	Date Modified	Modified By				Modifications
--	01-Nov-2019		Madhumita					
**************************************************************/
AS
BEGIN

	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	DECLARE @vCustId int;
	DECLARE @vConId int;

BEGIN TRY
		SET @pErrorMessage = ''
		
		SET @vCustId = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/ 
		SET @vConId = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_CONTACTS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/ 
		--SET @pCustId = 	@vCustId;												
		INSERT INTO BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER
				   (CUST_ID,
				   CUST_REFERENCE,
					CUST_ORG_Name,
					CUST_TELEPHONE,
					CFSID,
					
					--COMPANY_ID,
					--cust_Utility,
					CREATED_ON,
					CREATED_BY,
					UPDATED_ON,
					UPDATED_BY)
			 SELECT
					@vCustId,
					'CUS'+@vCustId,
					@pCustOrgName,
					@pTelephone,
					@pCFSID,
					--@pcompanyID,
					--@pCustUtility,
					GETDATE(),
					@ploggedonUser,
					GETDATE(),
					@ploggedonUser

		INSERT INTO BT_CONTACTS_MASTER
				   (con_contactID,
				   con_parentID,
				   con_parentType,
				   --con_fax,
					con_mobile,
					con_email,
					--con_CFSID,
					con_dateCreated,
					con_createdBy,
					con_lastUpdated,
					con_lastUpdatedBy,
					con_deleted)
			 SELECT
						@vConId,
						@vCustId,
						1,
						--@pFax,
						@pMobile,
						@pEmail,
						--@pContCFSID,
						GETDATE(),
						@ploggedonUser,
						GETDATE(),
						@ploggedonUser,
						'N' 

			SELECT @pCustref=CUST_REFERENCE 
			FROM BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER;
END TRY

BEGIN CATCH
		SELECT @pErrorMessage='Error Code: ' + CAST (ERROR_NUMBER() AS VARCHAR(10)) + ', Error: ' + ERROR_MESSAGE()
		RETURN 1
END CATCH	
END 







GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[usp_QMS_InitialDataLoad]  
(  
	@pQMSRef VARCHAR(50)  
)  
AS   
 BEGIN  
   DECLARE @mResult VARCHAR(500)=NULL;  
   DECLARE @AE_AEID INT;  
   DECLARE @internal_referance varchar(20);  
   DECLARE @vCustId int;  
   DECLARE @ploggedonUser INT;  
   Declare @NewCustRef Varchar(50);  
   DECLARE @vAddId int;  
   DECLARE @vConId int;  
     
   BEGIN TRY  
   
		SET @vCustId = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/
		
		SET @NewCustRef='CUST'+CONVERT(VARCHAR(MAX),@vCustId)   
   
		SELECT @ploggedonUser= INT_USR_ID 
		FROM MT_USERS 
		WHERE [USER_NAME]='AD03\QMS.User'  
    
		INSERT INTO BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER  
					(CUST_ID,  
					 CUST_REFERENCE,  
					 CUST_ORG_Name,  
					 CUST_TELEPHONE,  
					 CFSID,  
					 CREATED_ON,  
					 CREATED_BY,  
					 UPDATED_ON,  
					 UPDATED_BY,  
					 CUST_TYPE,  
					 CUST_DELETED,SOURCE_SYS)  
		SELECT  
					@vCustId,  
					@NewCustRef,  
					'',  
					'',  
					'',  
					GETDATE(),  
					@ploggedonUser,  
					GETDATE(),  
					@ploggedonUser,  
					'',  
					'N',  
					'QMS';  
  
		SET @vAddId = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ADDRESS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/   
    
                
		INSERT INTO BT_ADDRESS_MASTER  
				   ([ADDRESS_ID],
				   [parentID],
				   [addressLine1],
				   [addressLine2],
				   [addressLine3]  
				   ,[addressLine4],
				   [addressLine5] 
				   ,[addressLine6],
				   [addressLine7] 
				   ,[addressLine8]  
				   ,[postCode]
				   ,[easting] 
				   ,[Northing] 
				   ,[addressUsage]  
				   ,[dateCreated] 
				   ,[createdBy]
				   ,[lastUpdated]
				   ,[lastUpdatedBy]
				   ,[CFSID]
				   ,[deleted] 
				   ,[SOURCE_SYS]
                   )  
        SELECT  
					@vAddId
					,@vCustId
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,GETDATE()
					,@ploggedonUser
					,GETDATE()
					,@ploggedonUser
					,''
					,'N'
					,'QMS'
       
		SET @vConId = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_CONTACTS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/  
     
    
                
		INSERT INTO BT_CONTACTS_MASTER  
				   ([con_contactID],[con_parentID], [con_parentType],[con_surname] ,[con_forename]  
				   ,[con_personalTitle],[con_jobTitle] ,[con_telephone]  
				   ,[con_email] ,[con_mobile] ,[con_dateCreated],[con_createdBy],[con_lastUpdated],  
				   [con_lastUpdatedBy],[con_deleted],[con_CFSID],con_contactName ,SOURCE_SYS 
				   )  
      SELECT  
				 @vConId,@vCustId,1,'','','','',''  
				 ,'','',GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,'N','',  
				 ''   ,'QMS'  
				 
		UPDATE 	BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER SET CUST_PRIM_ADDRID = @vAddId, CUST_PRIM_CONTACTID = @vConId
		WHERE 	CUST_ID = @vCustId AND CUST_REFERENCE = @NewCustRef           
      
	  SET @AE_AEID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/  
  
   
	  SET @internal_referance=CONCAT('ENQ',@AE_AEID) ;  
  
	  INSERT INTO BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER   
				  ( AE_AEID,ae_address,AE_internalRef,AE_externalRef,AE_dateOrigin,AE_orderReceivedDate,AE_summaryDescription,AE_assignedToDept,AE_assignedToPerson,  
					AE_status,AE_type,AE_plotUnit,AE_networkDistributor,AE_metaModelType,AE_metaModelType2,AE_MPANRequested,AE_isTemplate,AE_sendToCFS,AE_customer,AE_createdBy,  
					AE_dateCreated,AE_lastUpdatedBy,AE_lastUpdated,AE_contact,AE_CustomerAddress,AE_companyID,AE_deleted,AE_taskInstruction,AE_useUplift,AE_Category,AE_stdCode,  
					AE_externalRef2  
					,AE_CustType,AE_externalRef3,AE_QuoteProjectDiscuss,AE_QuoteProvision  
					)  
	   VALUES  
			  (@AE_AEID,0,@internal_referance,null,getdate(),getdate(),REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE('', CHAR(13),' '), CHAR(10),' '),'&',' '),0,0,  
			   0,0,'',0,'T',1,'N','N','N',@vCustId,@ploggedonuser,  
			   GETDATE(),@ploggedonuser,getdate(),@vConId,@vAddId,1000,'N','Y','Y',-1,'NONE',@pQMSRef  
			   ,0,0,0,0);  
  
		 INSERT INTO MT_ENQUIRY_QUESTIONS    
		  (aeq_ae_aeid,aeq_POC_Voltage_LOV,aeq_HA_Voltage_LOV,aeq_GENERATION_LOV,aeq_TOW_REQUIRED_LOV,aeq_NUM_End_Connection_LOV,aeq_create_date,aeq_created_by,aeq_updated_by,aeq_updated_date)   
		 VALUES  
		  (@AE_AEID,0,0,0,0,0,GETDATE(),@ploggedonuser,@ploggedonuser,GETDATE());
		  
		  EXEC sp_ACT_addHIST_addEnquiry @AE_AEID, @ploggedonuser, 0, 0, 0, ''

		  UPDATE BT_Message SET Message_Status='W', QPID_Reference_Number=@internal_referance
		  Where QMS_Reference_Number=@pQMSRef
  
		SET @mResult=@internal_referance;  
   END TRY  
   BEGIN CATCH  
    
		SET @mResult=dbo.GetError();  
   END CATCH  
   SELECT @mResult AS RESULT;  
 END
GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[usp_QMS_InitialDataLoad_CRM]  
(  
	@pCRMRef VARCHAR(50)  
)  
AS   
 BEGIN  
   DECLARE @mResult VARCHAR(500)=NULL;  
   DECLARE @AE_AEID INT;  
   DECLARE @internal_referance varchar(20);  
   DECLARE @vCustId int;  
   DECLARE @ploggedonUser INT;  
   Declare @NewCustRef Varchar(50);  
   DECLARE @vAddId int;  
   DECLARE @vConId int;  
   DECLARE @msgTxt varchar(500);

   SET TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL READ UNCOMMITTED

   BEGIN TRY  
		--WAITFOR DELAY '00:00:02';
		SET @msgTxt = 'BEGIN INIT LOAD'+CONVERT( VARCHAR(24), GETDATE(), 121)+', '
		SET @vCustId = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/
		
		SET @NewCustRef='CUST'+CONVERT(VARCHAR(MAX),@vCustId)   
   
		SELECT @ploggedonUser= INT_USR_ID 
		FROM MT_USERS 
		WITH (NOLOCK)
		WHERE [USER_NAME]='AD03\CRM.User'  
    
		INSERT INTO BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER  
					(CUST_ID,  
					 CUST_REFERENCE,  
					 CUST_ORG_Name,  
					 CUST_TELEPHONE,  
					 CFSID,  
					 CREATED_ON,  
					 CREATED_BY,  
					 UPDATED_ON,  
					 UPDATED_BY,  
					 CUST_TYPE,  
					 CUST_DELETED,SOURCE_SYS)  
		SELECT  
					@vCustId,  
					@NewCustRef,  
					'',  
					'',  
					'',  
					GETDATE(),  
					@ploggedonUser,  
					GETDATE(),  
					@ploggedonUser,  
					'',  
					'N',  
					'CRM';  
  
		SET @vAddId = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ADDRESS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/   
    
                
		INSERT INTO BT_ADDRESS_MASTER  
				   ([ADDRESS_ID],
				   [parentID],
				   [addressLine1],
				   [addressLine2],
				   [addressLine3]  
				   ,[addressLine4],
				   [addressLine5] 
				   ,[addressLine6],
				   [addressLine7] 
				   ,[addressLine8]  
				   ,[postCode]
				   ,[easting] 
				   ,[Northing] 
				   ,[addressUsage]  
				   ,[dateCreated] 
				   ,[createdBy]
				   ,[lastUpdated]
				   ,[lastUpdatedBy]
				   ,[CFSID]
				   ,[deleted] 
				   ,[SOURCE_SYS]
                   )  
        SELECT  
					@vAddId
					,@vCustId
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,''
					,GETDATE()
					,@ploggedonUser
					,GETDATE()
					,@ploggedonUser
					,''
					,'N'
					,'CRM'
       
		SET @vConId = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_CONTACTS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/  
     
    
                
		INSERT INTO BT_CONTACTS_MASTER  
				   ([con_contactID],[con_parentID], [con_parentType],[con_surname] ,[con_forename]  
				   ,[con_personalTitle],[con_jobTitle] ,[con_telephone]  
				   ,[con_email] ,[con_mobile] ,[con_dateCreated],[con_createdBy],[con_lastUpdated],  
				   [con_lastUpdatedBy],[con_deleted],[con_CFSID],con_contactName ,SOURCE_SYS 
				   )  
      SELECT  
				 @vConId,@vCustId,1,'','','','',''  
				 ,'','',GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,'N','',  
				 ''   ,'CRM'  
				 
		UPDATE 	BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER SET CUST_PRIM_ADDRID = @vAddId, CUST_PRIM_CONTACTID = @vConId
		WHERE 	CUST_ID = @vCustId AND CUST_REFERENCE = @NewCustRef           
      
	  SET @AE_AEID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/  
  
   
	  SET @internal_referance=CONCAT('ENQ',@AE_AEID) ;  
  
	  INSERT INTO BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER   
				  ( AE_AEID,ae_address,AE_internalRef,AE_externalRef,AE_dateOrigin,AE_orderReceivedDate,AE_summaryDescription,AE_assignedToDept,AE_assignedToPerson,  
					AE_status,AE_type,AE_plotUnit,AE_networkDistributor,AE_metaModelType,AE_metaModelType2,AE_MPANRequested,AE_isTemplate,AE_sendToCFS,AE_customer,AE_createdBy,  
					AE_dateCreated,AE_lastUpdatedBy,AE_lastUpdated,AE_contact,AE_CustomerAddress,AE_companyID,AE_deleted,AE_taskInstruction,AE_useUplift,AE_Category,AE_stdCode,  
					AE_externalRef2  
					,AE_CustType,AE_externalRef3,AE_QuoteProjectDiscuss,AE_QuoteProvision  
					)  
	   VALUES  
			  (@AE_AEID,0,@internal_referance,null,getdate(),getdate(),REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE('', CHAR(13),' '), CHAR(10),' '),'&',' '),0,0,  
			   0,0,'',0,'T',1,'N','N','N',@vCustId,@ploggedonuser,  
			   GETDATE(),@ploggedonuser,getdate(),@vConId,@vAddId,1000,'N','Y','Y',-1,'NONE',@pCRMRef  
			   ,0,0,0,0);  
  
		 INSERT INTO MT_ENQUIRY_QUESTIONS    
		  (aeq_ae_aeid,aeq_POC_Voltage_LOV,aeq_HA_Voltage_LOV,aeq_GENERATION_LOV,aeq_TOW_REQUIRED_LOV,aeq_NUM_End_Connection_LOV,aeq_create_date,aeq_created_by,aeq_updated_by,aeq_updated_date)   
		 VALUES  
		  (@AE_AEID,0,0,0,0,0,GETDATE(),@ploggedonuser,@ploggedonuser,GETDATE());
		  
		  EXEC sp_ACT_addHIST_addEnquiry @AE_AEID, @ploggedonuser, 0, 0, 0, ''
		 
		  --Create default standards
		  declare @sourceType varchar(50)
		  SELECT
			@sourceType=source
		  FROM
			BT_Message
		  WITH (NOLOCK)
		  WHERE
			QMS_Reference_Number=@pCRMRef
		  IF @sourceType = 'CRM_SmallWork'
		  BEGIN
			IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM BT_ACT_STANDARDS_CRM_STG WHERE QMS_REF= @pCRMRef)
			BEGIN
				declare @currentDt varchar(50)
				set @currentDt = convert(varchar,getdate(),121)
				EXEC SP_ADD_STANDARDS  @ploggedonuser,@internal_referance, 294, @currentDt, 5000,1
			END

			--Update task category items
			update BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS_CRM_STG set TCI_nonContestValQty=TCI_contestValQty where qms_ref=@pCRMRef

		  END

		  --UPDATE BT_Message SET Message_Status='W', QPID_Reference_Number=@internal_referance
		  --Where QMS_Reference_Number=@pCRMRef and Message_Status!='P'
		
		IF exists(select parent_techType_name,child_techType_name,ed.device_type,ed.QMS_REF
					from BT_EquipmentDetails_CRM_STG ed
					LEFT JOIN BT_Equipment_Techtype_Mapping_CRM_STG edm 
					on REPLACE(UPPER(edm.equipment_name),' ','') = REPLACE(UPPER(ed.device_type),' ','') 
					and edm.lov_type='sysTechnologyType'
					where ed.QMS_REF=@pCRMRef
					AND (edm.parent_techType_name IS NULL or edm.child_techType_name IS NULL))
			BEGIN
				UPDATE BT_Message 
				SET Message_Status='F',
					QPID_Reference_Number=@internal_referance, 
					Error_Description='Invalid Equipment, Technology type does not exists in QPID'
				Where QMS_Reference_Number=@pCRMRef 
					  and Message_Status!='P'
			END
		ELSE
			BEGIN
				UPDATE BT_Message 
				SET Message_Status='W', 
					QPID_Reference_Number=@internal_referance
				Where QMS_Reference_Number=@pCRMRef 
					  and Message_Status!='P'
			END
		
		SET @mResult=@internal_referance;  
		SET @msgTxt = @msgTxt + @mResult + ' END INIT LOAD '+CONVERT( VARCHAR(24), GETDATE(), 121)
		exec usp_CreateEntryForExecutionTime @pCRMRef,@msgTxt;
		
		/*IF @sourceType = 'CRM_MajorWork'
		BEGIN
			exec usp_QMS_FinalDataLoad_CRM_MajorWork
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			exec usp_QMS_FinalDataLoad_CRM_SmallWork
		END*/
   END TRY  
   BEGIN CATCH  
    
		SET @mResult=dbo.GetError();  
		SET @msgTxt = @msgTxt + @mResult + ' END INIT LOAD '+CONVERT( VARCHAR(24), GETDATE(), 121)
		exec usp_CreateEntryForExecutionTime @pCRMRef,@msgTxt;
   END CATCH  
   SELECT @mResult AS RESULT;  
   
 END
GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

-- ---- usp_QMS_InitialDataLoad_CRM (4 sites) with QPID_OPT V4-09

				 ,'','',GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,'N','',  
				 ''   ,'CRM'  
				 
		UPDATE 	BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER SET CUST_PRIM_ADDRID = @vAddId, CUST_PRIM_CONTACTID = @vConId
		WHERE 	CUST_ID = @vCustId AND CUST_REFERENCE = @NewCustRef           
      
	  SET @AE_AEID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/  
  
   
	  SET @internal_referance=CONCAT('ENQ',@AE_AEID) ;  
  
	  INSERT INTO BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER   
				  ( AE_AEID,ae_address,AE_internalRef,AE_externalRef,AE_dateOrigin,AE_orderReceivedDate,AE_summaryDescription,AE_assignedToDept,AE_assignedToPerson,  
					AE_status,AE_type,AE_plotUnit,AE_networkDistributor,AE_metaModelType,AE_metaModelType2,AE_MPANRequested,AE_isTemplate,AE_sendToCFS,AE_customer,AE_createdBy,  
					AE_dateCreated,AE_lastUpdatedBy,AE_lastUpdated,AE_contact,AE_CustomerAddress,AE_companyID,AE_deleted,AE_taskInstruction,AE_useUplift,AE_Category,AE_stdCode,  
					AE_externalRef2  
					,AE_CustType,AE_externalRef3,AE_QuoteProjectDiscuss,AE_QuoteProvision  
					)  
	   VALUES  
			  (@AE_AEID,0,@internal_referance,null,getdate(),getdate(),REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE('', CHAR(13),' '), CHAR(10),' '),'&',' '),0,0,  
			   0,0,'',0,'T',1,'N','N','N',@vCustId,@ploggedonuser,  
			   GETDATE(),@ploggedonuser,getdate(),@vConId,@vAddId,1000,'N','Y','Y',-1,'NONE',@pCRMRef  
			   ,0,0,0,0);  
  
		 INSERT INTO MT_ENQUIRY_QUESTIONS    
		  (aeq_ae_aeid,aeq_POC_Voltage_LOV,aeq_HA_Voltage_LOV,aeq_GENERATION_LOV,aeq_TOW_REQUIRED_LOV,aeq_NUM_End_Connection_LOV,aeq_create_date,aeq_created_by,aeq_updated_by,aeq_updated_date)   
		 VALUES  
		  (@AE_AEID,0,0,0,0,0,GETDATE(),@ploggedonuser,@ploggedonuser,GETDATE());
		  
		  EXEC sp_ACT_addHIST_addEnquiry @AE_AEID, @ploggedonuser, 0, 0, 0, ''
		 
		  --Create default standards
		  declare @sourceType varchar(50)
		  SELECT
			@sourceType=source
		  FROM
			BT_Message
		  WITH (NOLOCK)
		  WHERE
			QMS_Reference_Number=@pCRMRef
		  IF @sourceType = 'CRM_SmallWork'
		  BEGIN
			IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT * FROM BT_ACT_STANDARDS_CRM_STG WHERE QMS_REF= @pCRMRef)
			BEGIN
				declare @currentDt varchar(50)
				set @currentDt = convert(varchar,getdate(),121)
				EXEC SP_ADD_STANDARDS  @ploggedonuser,@internal_referance, 294, @currentDt, 5000,1
			END

			--Update task category items
			update BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS_CRM_STG set TCI_nonContestValQty=TCI_contestValQty where qms_ref=@pCRMRef

		  END

		  --UPDATE BT_Message SET Message_Status='W', QPID_Reference_Number=@internal_referance
		  --Where QMS_Reference_Number=@pCRMRef and Message_Status!='P'
		
		IF exists(select parent_techType_name,child_techType_name,ed.device_type,ed.QMS_REF
					from BT_EquipmentDetails_CRM_STG ed
					LEFT JOIN BT_Equipment_Techtype_Mapping_CRM_STG edm 
					on REPLACE(UPPER(edm.equipment_name),' ','') = REPLACE(UPPER(ed.device_type),' ','') 
					and edm.lov_type='sysTechnologyType'
					where ed.QMS_REF=@pCRMRef
					AND (edm.parent_techType_name IS NULL or edm.child_techType_name IS NULL))
			BEGIN
				UPDATE BT_Message 
				SET Message_Status='F',
					QPID_Reference_Number=@internal_referance, 
					Error_Description='Invalid Equipment, Technology type does not exists in QPID'
				Where QMS_Reference_Number=@pCRMRef 
					  and Message_Status!='P'
			END
		ELSE
			BEGIN
				UPDATE BT_Message 
				SET Message_Status='W', 
					QPID_Reference_Number=@internal_referance
				Where QMS_Reference_Number=@pCRMRef 
					  and Message_Status!='P'
			END
		
		SET @mResult=@internal_referance;  
		SET @msgTxt = @msgTxt + @mResult + ' END INIT LOAD '+CONVERT( VARCHAR(24), GETDATE(), 121)
		exec usp_CreateEntryForExecutionTime @pCRMRef,@msgTxt;
		
		/*IF @sourceType = 'CRM_MajorWork'
		BEGIN
			exec usp_QMS_FinalDataLoad_CRM_MajorWork
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			exec usp_QMS_FinalDataLoad_CRM_SmallWork
		END*/
   END TRY  
   BEGIN CATCH  
    
		SET @mResult=dbo.GetError();  
		IF XACT_STATE() = -1 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION; -- QPID_OPT V4-09: a log write in a doomed transaction raises 3930 and hides the real error.
		SET @msgTxt = @msgTxt + @mResult + ' END INIT LOAD '+CONVERT( VARCHAR(24), GETDATE(), 121)
		exec usp_CreateEntryForExecutionTime @pCRMRef,@msgTxt;
   END CATCH  
   SELECT @mResult AS RESULT;  
   
 END
GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[CDMS_ACT_uspAddAddress]
	@ploggedonUser				VARCHAR(25),
	@pCustRef					VARCHAR(50),
	--@pCustId						INT,
	--@pCustOrgName 				VARCHAR(50),
	--@pTelephone 				VARCHAR(50)= NULL,
	@pCustUtility 				INT,
	@pFreeText					VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pSubBuilding				VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pBuilding					VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pSubStreet					VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pStreet					VARCHAR(50),
	@pDDLocality				VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pDLocality					VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pTown						VARCHAR(50),
	@pCountryID					INT =1643,
	@pPostCode					VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pEasting					VARCHAR(10) = NULL,
	@pNorthing					VARCHAR(10) = NULL,
	@pCFSID						INT = NULL,
	@pErrorMessage				VARCHAR(5000) OUTPUT 

/**************************************************************											
--	Procedure Name	:	CDMS_ACT_uspAddAddress
--	Description		:	Insert Customer Address Details
--	Parameters		:	
						
						@pErrorMessage - Output parameter to return the error message

--	Return Value	:	0	Success			
--						1	Failure

--	Date Modified	Modified By				Modifications
--	05-Nov-2019		Madhumita					
**************************************************************/
AS
BEGIN

	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	DECLARE @vAddId int;
	DECLARE @vConId int;
	DECLARE @vCustId int;

BEGIN TRY
		SET @pErrorMessage = ''
		
		SET @vAddId = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ADDRESS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/ 
		SELECT @vCustId = CUST_ID  FROM BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER where CUST_REFERENCE = @pCustRef
														
		INSERT INTO BT_ADDRESS_MASTER
				   ([ADDRESS_ID],[parentID],[addressLine1] ,[addressLine2] ,[addressLine3]
				   ,[addressLine4],[addressLine5] ,[addressLine6],[addressLine7] ,[addressLine8]
				   ,[postCode] ,[Country] ,[easting] ,[Northing] ,[addressUsage]
				   ,[dateCreated] ,[createdBy],[lastUpdated],[lastUpdatedBy] ,[CFSID]
				   )
					 SELECT
					@vAddId,@vCustId,@pFreeText,@pSubBuilding,@pBuilding,
					@pSubStreet,@pStreet,@pDDLocality,@pDLocality,@pTown,
					@pPostCode,@pCountryID,@pEasting,@pNorthing,@pCustUtility,
					GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,@pCFSID





			

		RETURN 0
END TRY

BEGIN CATCH
		SELECT @pErrorMessage='Error Code: ' + CAST (ERROR_NUMBER() AS VARCHAR(10)) + ', Error: ' + ERROR_MESSAGE()
		RETURN 1
END CATCH	
END 







GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

		SET @pErrorMessage = ''

		SELECT @vCustId = CUST_ID  FROM BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER where CUST_REFERENCE = @pCustRef

					,@pEmail,@pMobile,GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,'N',@pCFSID





			

		RETURN 0
END TRY

BEGIN CATCH

	'T',

	@userID,

	,'N'

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[PRC_ACT__Add_Update_BulkCTKCorroAddress] 
@activityID int,
@addr_addressID int,
@addr_parentID int,
@addr_Line1 varchar(50),
@addr_Line2 varchar(50),
@addr_Line3 varchar(50),
@addr_Line4 varchar(50),
@addr_Line5 varchar(50),
@addr_Line6 varchar(50),
@addr_Line7 varchar(50),
@addr_Line8 varchar(50),
@addr_Line9 varchar(50),
@addr_PostCode varchar(50),
@addr_contactName varchar(100),
--@addr_contactTel1 varchar(50),
--@addr_contactTel2 varchar(50),
--@addr_useAsMailingAddr varchar(1),
--@addr_Northing varchar(10),
--@addr_Easting varchar(10),
@addr_PAFKey varchar(50),
@userID int
AS

Begin

Declare @mResult varchar(1000)='';

Begin Tran
Begin Try

IF ISNULL(@addr_addressID,0) = 0 
BEGIN

SET @addr_addressID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ADDRESS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/

	INSERT BT_ADDRESS_MASTER(
		ADDRESS_ID,
		parentID,
		addressLine1,
		addressLine2,
		addressLine3,
		addressLine4,
		addressLine5,
		addressLine6,
		addressLine7,
		addressLine8,
		addressLine9,
		PostCode,
		addr_contactName ,
		--addr_contactTel1 ,
		--addr_contactTel2 ,
		--useAsMailingAddr ,
		dateCreated,
		createdBy,
		lastUpdated,
		lastUpdatedBy,
		deleted,
		--Northing,
		--Easting,
		PAFKey )
	VALUES(
		@addr_addressID,
		@addr_parentID,
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line1)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line2)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line3)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line4)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line5)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line6)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line7)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line8)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line9)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_PostCode)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_contactName)) ,
		--LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_contactTel1)) ,
		--LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_contactTel2)) ,
		--@addr_useAsMailingAddr ,
		getDate(),
		@userID,
		getDate(),
		@userID,
		'N',
		--@addr_Northing,
		--@addr_Easting,
		@addr_PAFKey
	)
		UPDATE act
	SET act.AE_correspondenceAddress = @addr_addressID, AE_lastUpdatedBy = @userID,AE_lastUpdated=getDate()
	FROM [dbo].BT_CTK_HEADER act
	WHERE act.AE_AEID = @activityID
END
ELSE
	BEGIN
		UPDATE BT_ADDRESS_MASTER
		SET 	
			addressLine1 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line1)),
			addressLine2 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line2)),
			addressLine3 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line3)),
			addressLine4 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line4)),
			addressLine5 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line5)),
			addressLine6 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line6)),
			addressLine7 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line7)),
			addressLine8 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line8)),
			addressLine9 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line9)),
			PostCode = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_PostCode)),
			addr_contactName = @addr_contactName ,
			--addr_contactTel1 = @addr_contactTel1 ,
			--addr_contactTel2 = @addr_contactTel2 ,
			--useAsMailingAddr = @addr_useAsMailingAddr ,
			lastUpdated = getDate(),
			lastUpdatedBy = @userID,
			Deleted = 'N',
			--Northing = @addr_Northing,
			--Easting = @addr_Easting,
			PAFKey = @addr_PAFKey,
			parentID=@addr_parentID
		WHERE address_ID = @addr_addressID

		
	UPDATE act
	SET AE_lastUpdatedBy = @userID,AE_lastUpdated=getDate()
	FROM [dbo].BT_CTK_HEADER act
	WHERE act.AE_AEID = @activityID

	END
	--   Select * from BT_ADDRESS_MASTER where address_ID=8670783
	--   Select * from BT_ADDRESS_MASTER where address_ID=8671544
	
commit tran 
	set @mResult='';
End try
Begin catch
	Rollback tran
	Set @mResult=dbo.GetError();
end catch
select @mResult

ENd
GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;

@addr_Easting varchar(10),
@addr_Northing varchar(10),
@addr_CFSID int,
@addr_descr varchar(255),
@addr_Notes varchar(255),

@userID int
AS
Begin

Begin Tran
Begin Try

IF ISNULL(@addr_addressID,0) = 0 
BEGIN

SET @addr_addressID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ADDRESS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/

	ADDRESS_ID,
	parentID,
	siteName,
	addressLine1,
	addressLine2,
	addressLine3,
	addressLine4,
	addressLine5,
	addressLine6,

	@addr_addressID,
	@addr_parentID,
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_siteName)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line1)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line2)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line3)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line4)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line5)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line6)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line7)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line8)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line9)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_PostCode)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Country)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_MPANCore)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_PAFKey)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Easting)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Northing)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_CFSID)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_descr)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Notes)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Usage)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Category)),
	getDate(),
	@userID,
	getDate(),
	@userID,
	'N',
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_contactName)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_contactTel1))
)
-- now update cdms_act_activityevent with new address id
	UPDATE act
	SET act.AE_address = @addr_addressID
	FROM [dbo].BT_CTK_HEADER act
	WHERE act.AE_AEID = @activityID
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	UPDATE BT_ADDRESS_MASTER SET 	
	siteName = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_siteName)),
	addressLine1 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line1)),
	addressLine2 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line2)),
	addressLine3 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line3)),
	addressLine4 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line4)),
	addressLine5 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line5)),
	addressLine6 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line6)),
	addressLine7 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line7)),
	addressLine8 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line8)),
	addressLine9 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line9)),
	PostCode = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_PostCode)),
	Country = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Country)),
	MPANCore = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_MPANCore)),
	PAFKey = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_PAFKey)),
	easting = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Easting)),
	northing = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Northing)),
	CFSID = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_CFSID)),
	description = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_descr)),
	note = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Notes)),
	addressUsage = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Usage)),
	addressCategory = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Category)),
	lastUpdated = getDate(),
	lastUpdatedBy = @userID,
	Deleted = 'N',
	addr_contactName = @addr_contactName,
	addr_contactTel1 = @addr_contactTel1,
	parentID=@addr_parentID
	WHERE ADDRESS_ID = @addr_addressID
END

	set @mResult='';

Begin catch
	Rollback tran

@addr_PAFKey varchar(50),
@userID int
AS

Begin

Begin Tran
Begin Try

IF ISNULL(@addr_addressID,0) = 0 
BEGIN

			PostCode = LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_PostCode)),
			addr_contactName = @addr_contactName ,
			addr_contactTel1 = @addr_contactTel1 ,
			addr_contactTel2 = @addr_contactTel2 ,
			--useAsMailingAddr = @addr_useAsMailingAddr ,
			lastUpdated = getDate(),
			lastUpdatedBy = @userID,
			Deleted = 'N',
			Northing = @addr_Northing,
			Easting = @addr_Easting,
			PAFKey = @addr_PAFKey,
			parentID=@addr_parentID
		WHERE address_ID = @addr_addressID

	END

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[PRC_ACT_editContactDetails]

@activityID int,

@own_addressID int,
@own_contactName varchar(250),
@own_Line1 varchar(50),
@own_Line2 varchar(50),
@own_Line3 varchar(50),
@own_Line4 varchar(50),
@own_Line5 varchar(50),
@own_Line6 varchar(50),
@own_Line7 varchar(50),
@own_Line8 varchar(50),
@own_Line9 varchar(50),
@own_PostCode varchar(50),
@own_PAFKey varchar(50),
@own_Easting varchar(50),
@own_Northing varchar(50),

@alt_addressID int,
@alt_contactName varchar(250),
@alt_Line1 varchar(50),
@alt_Line2 varchar(50),
@alt_Line3 varchar(50),
@alt_Line4 varchar(50),
@alt_Line5 varchar(50),
@alt_Line6 varchar(50),
@alt_Line7 varchar(50),
@alt_Line8 varchar(50),
@alt_Line9 varchar(50),
@alt_PostCode varchar(50),
@alt_PAFKey varchar(50),
@alt_TelNumber varchar(50),
@alt_PONumber varchar(50),
@alt_Easting varchar(50),
@alt_Northing varchar(50),
@userID int,
@alt_coeInvonum varchar(50),
@alt_coePaymentDate datetime=null,
@alt_coeInvoissueDate datetime=null,
@alt_emailAdd varchar (50)

AS


Declare @mResult varchar(1000)='';

Begin Tran
Begin Try
DECLARE @newOwnAddressId INT
DECLARE @newAltAddressId INT
DECLARE @ownLength INT
DECLARE @altLength INT
DECLARE @EX_ID INT

SET @newOwnAddressId = 0
SET @newAltAddressId = 0

SET @ownLength =  LEN(	ISNULL(@own_contactName,'') + 
						ISNULL(@own_Line1,'') +
						ISNULL(@own_Line2,'') +
						ISNULL(@own_Line3,'') +
						ISNULL(@own_Line4,'') +
						ISNULL(@own_Line5,'') +
						ISNULL(@own_Line6,'') +
						ISNULL(@own_Line7,'') +
						ISNULL(@own_Line8,'') +
						ISNULL(@own_Line9,'') +
						ISNULL(@own_PostCode,'') +
						ISNULL(@own_PAFKey,'') );

SET @altLength =  LEN(	ISNULL(@alt_contactName,'') + 
						ISNULL(@alt_Line1,'') +
						ISNULL(@alt_Line2,'') +
						ISNULL(@alt_Line3,'') +
						ISNULL(@alt_Line4,'') +
						ISNULL(@alt_Line5,'') +
						ISNULL(@alt_Line6,'') +
						ISNULL(@alt_Line7,'') +
						ISNULL(@alt_Line8,'') +
						ISNULL(@alt_Line9,'') +
						ISNULL(@alt_PostCode,'') +
						ISNULL(@alt_PAFKey,'') +
						ISNULL(@alt_TelNumber,'') +
						ISNULL(@alt_PONumber,'') +
						ISNULL(@alt_Easting,'') +
						ISNULL(@alt_Northing,'') +
						ISNULL(@alt_coeInvonum,'') +						
						ISNULL(@alt_emailAdd,''));

						
-- owner / occupier address is new
 SELECT @activityID=AE_AEID FROM BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER WHERE AE_internalRef=Concat('ENQ',@activityID)

IF ISNULL(@own_addressID,0) = 0 --AND @ownLength > 0
BEGIN
SET @own_addressID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ADDRESS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[BT_ADDRESS_MASTER]
	(	[ADDRESS_ID],parentID,
		[addr_contactName],
		[addressLine1], 
		[addressLine2], 
		[addressLine3], 
		[addressLine4], 
		[addressLine5], 
		[addressLine6], 
		[addressLine7], 
		[addressLine8], 
		[addressLine9], 
		[postCode], 
		[PAFKey],
		[easting],
		[northing],
		[dateCreated], 
		[createdBy], 
		[lastUpdated], 
		[lastUpdatedBy], 
		[deleted]	 )
	VALUES
	(	@own_addressID,@activityID,
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_contactName)), 
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line1)), 
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line2)), 
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line3)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line4)), 
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line5)), 
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line6)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line7)), 
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line8)), 
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line9)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_PostCode)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_PAFKey)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Easting)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Northing)),
		GETDATE(), 
		@userId, 
		GETDATE(), 
		@userId, 
		'N' )

	--SELECT @newOwnAddressId = @own_addressID

	-- now update cdms_act_activityevent with new address id
	----UPDATE act
	----SET act.AE_address = @newOwnAddressId
	----FROM [dbo].[BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER] act
	----WHERE act.AE_AEID = @activityID
	UPDATE act
	SET act.[AE_correspondenceAddress] = @own_addressID
	FROM [dbo].BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER act
	WHERE act.AE_AEID = @activityID
END
ELSE --IF ISNULL(@own_addressID,0) > 0 AND @ownLength > 0
BEGIN
	UPDATE [dbo].[BT_ADDRESS_MASTER]
	SET 	
		addr_contactName = LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_contactName)),
		addressLine1 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line1)),
		addressLine2 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line2)),
		addressLine3 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line3)),
		addressLine4 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line4)),
		addressLine5 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line5)),
		addressLine6 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line6)),
		addressLine7 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line7)),
		addressLine8 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line8)),
		addressLine9 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Line9)),
		PostCode = LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_PostCode)),
		PAFKey = LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_PAFKey)),
		easting = LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Easting)),
		northing = LTRIM(RTRIM(@own_Northing)),
		lastUpdated = getDate(),
		lastUpdatedBy = @UserID
	WHERE ADDRESS_ID = @own_addressID

	UPDATE act
	SET act.[AE_correspondenceAddress] = @own_addressID
	FROM [dbo].BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER act
	WHERE act.AE_AEID = @activityID
END

-- alternative address is new
IF ISNULL(@alt_addressID,0) = 0 --AND @altLength > 0
BEGIN
SET @alt_addressID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ADDRESS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/

	INSERT INTO [dbo].[BT_ADDRESS_MASTER]
	(	[ADDRESS_ID],parentID,
		[addr_contactName],
		[addressLine1], 
		[addressLine2], 
		[addressLine3], 
		[addressLine4], 
		[addressLine5], 
		[addressLine6], 
		[addressLine7], 
		[addressLine8], 
		[addressLine9], 
		[postCode], 
		[PAFKey],
		[addr_contactTel1],
		[easting],
		[northing],
		[dateCreated], 
		[createdBy], 
		[lastUpdated], 
		[lastUpdatedBy], 
		[deleted]
			 )
	VALUES
	(	@alt_addressID,@activityID,
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_contactName)), 
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line1)), 
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line2)), 
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line3)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line4)), 
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line5)), 
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line6)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line7)), 
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line8)), 
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line9)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_PostCode)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_PAFKey)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_TelNumber)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Easting)),
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Northing)),
		GETDATE(), 
		@userId, 
		GETDATE(), 
		@userId, 
		'N')

	SELECT @newAltAddressId = @alt_addressID
	
	
	
	-- now insert cdms_act_ae_extra with new address id (not forgetting the po number field)
	Update CDMS_ACT_AE_EXTRA Set EX_deleted='Y' WHERE [EX_AEID]=@activityID
	SET @EX_ID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_CDMS_ACT_AE_EXTRA; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/

	INSERT INTO [dbo].[CDMS_ACT_AE_EXTRA]
	(	[EX_ID],
		[EX_AEID], 
		[EX_clawBackAmount], 
		[EX_dateCreated], 
		[EX_dateCreatedBy], 
		[EX_lastUpdated], 
		[EX_lastUpdatedBy], 
		[EX_deleted],
		[EX_PO_RefNumber], 
		[EX_altContactAddress]  )
	VALUES
	(	@EX_ID,
		@activityID, 
		0, 
		GETDATE(), 
		@userId, 
		GETDATE(), 
		@userId, 
		'N', 
		LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_PONumber)), 
		@newAltAddressId)

END
ELSE --IF ISNULL(@alt_addressID,0) > 0 AND @altLength > 0
BEGIN
	UPDATE [dbo].[BT_ADDRESS_MASTER]
	SET 	
		addr_contactName = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_contactName)),
		addressLine1 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line1)),
		addressLine2 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line2)),
		addressLine3 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line3)),
		addressLine4 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line4)),
		addressLine5 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line5)),
		addressLine6 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line6)),
		addressLine7 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line7)),
		addressLine8 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line8)),
		addressLine9 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Line9)),
		PostCode = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_PostCode)),
		PAFKey = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_PAFKey)),
		addr_contactTel1 = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_TelNumber)),
		easting = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Easting)),
		northing = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_Northing)),
		lastUpdated = getDate(),
		coeInvonum = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_coeInvonum)),
		coeInvoissueDate = @alt_coeInvoissueDate,
		coePaymentDate = @alt_coePaymentDate,
		emailAdd = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_emailAdd)),	
		lastUpdatedBy = @UserID
	WHERE ADDRESS_ID = @alt_addressID

	-- now update related cdms_act_ae_extra record with new po number value
	UPDATE ex
	SET ex.[EX_PO_RefNumber] = LTRIM(RTRIM(@alt_PONumber))
	FROM [dbo].[cdms_act_ae_extra] ex
	WHERE ex.EX_AEID = @activityID
	AND ex.[EX_altContactAddress] = @alt_addressID

END

commit tran 
	set @mResult='';
End try
Begin catch
	Rollback tran
	Set @mResult=dbo.GetError();
end catch
select @mResult
GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_ADD_CUST_ADDRESS]
	@ploggedonUser				VARCHAR(25),
	@pCustRef					VARCHAR(50),
	@pAddrUSage 				VARCHAR(10),
	@pFreeText					VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pSubBuilding				VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pBuilding					VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pSubStreet					VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pStreet					VARCHAR(50),
	@pDDLocality				VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pDLocality					VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pTown						VARCHAR(50),
	@pPostCode					VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pEasting					VARCHAR(10) = NULL,
	@pNorthing					VARCHAR(10) = NULL,
	@pCFSID						int = NULL,
	@pErrorMessage				VARCHAR(5000) OUTPUT 
AS
BEGIN

	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	DECLARE @vAddId int;
	DECLARE @vConId int;
	DECLARE @vCustId int;

BEGIN TRY
		SET @pErrorMessage = ''
		
		SET @vAddId = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ADDRESS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/ 
		SELECT @vCustId = CUST_ID  FROM BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER where CUST_REFERENCE = @pCustRef
														
		INSERT INTO BT_ADDRESS_MASTER
				   ([ADDRESS_ID],[parentID],[addressLine1] ,[addressLine2] ,[addressLine3]
				   ,[addressLine4],[addressLine5] ,[addressLine6],[addressLine7] ,[addressLine8]
				   ,[postCode],[easting] ,[Northing] ,[addressUsage]
				   ,[dateCreated] ,[createdBy],[lastUpdated],[lastUpdatedBy] ,[CFSID],deleted
				   )
					 SELECT
					@vAddId,@vCustId,@pFreeText,@pSubBuilding,@pBuilding,
					@pSubStreet,@pStreet,@pDDLocality,@pDLocality,@pTown,
					@pPostCode,@pEasting,@pNorthing,@pAddrUSage,
					GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,@pCFSID,'N'

	  SELECT @vAddId;



			

		RETURN 0
END TRY

BEGIN CATCH
		SELECT @pErrorMessage='Error Code: ' + CAST (ERROR_NUMBER() AS VARCHAR(10)) + ', Error: ' + ERROR_MESSAGE()
		RETURN 1
END CATCH	
END 







GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

IF @@TRANCOUNT = 0 SET NOEXEC ON;

SET ANSI_NULLS ON

GO




ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_ADD_CUST_CONTACT]
	@ploggedonUser				VARCHAR(25),
	@pCustRef					VARCHAR(50),
	@pJob					VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pMobile				VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pTele					VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pEmail					VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pSurName				VARCHAR(50),
	@pForeName				VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pTitle				    VARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@pCFSID						INT = NULL,
	@pErrorMessage				VARCHAR(5000) OUTPUT 

AS
BEGIN

	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	--DECLARE @vAddId int;
	DECLARE @vConId int;
	DECLARE @vCustId int;

BEGIN TRY
		SET @pErrorMessage = ''
		
		SET @vConId = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_CONTACTS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/
		SELECT @vCustId = CUST_ID  FROM BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER where CUST_REFERENCE = @pCustRef;
		
														
		INSERT INTO BT_CONTACTS_MASTER
				   ([con_contactID],[con_parentID], [con_parentType],[con_surname] ,[con_forename]
				   ,[con_personalTitle],[con_jobTitle] ,[con_telephone]
				   ,[con_email] ,[con_mobile] ,[con_dateCreated],[con_createdBy],[con_lastUpdated],
				   [con_lastUpdatedBy],[con_deleted],[con_CFSID],con_contactName
				   )
					 SELECT
					@vConId,@vCustId,1,@pSurName,@pForeName,COALESCE(@pTitle,'NA'),@pJob,@pTele
					,@pEmail,@pMobile,GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,'N',@pCFSID,
					COALESCE(@pTitle,'NA')+' '+@pForeName+' '+@pSurName

		SELECT @vConId;

END TRY

BEGIN CATCH
		SELECT @pErrorMessage='Error Code: ' + CAST (ERROR_NUMBER() AS VARCHAR(10)) + ', Error: ' + ERROR_MESSAGE()
		RETURN 1
END CATCH	
END 

GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

IF @@TRANCOUNT = 0 SET NOEXEC ON;

-- =============================================
-- Author:		TCS
-- Create date: 28-Nov-2019
-- Modified  by :Mousumi
-- Modified Date : 25/04/2020
-- Description:	Add Enquiry
-- =============================================
ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_ADD_ENQUIRY_V1]
(@ploggedonuser INT,
@external_referance varchar(250)=NULL,
@enquiry_description varchar(250)=NULL,
@assinged_to_dept varchar(250)=NULL,
@assinged_to_person varchar(250)=NULL,
@status varchar(250)=NULL,
@source varchar(250)=NULL,
@plot varchar(250)=NULL,
@network_distributor varchar(250)=NULL,
@poc_volt varchar(250)=NULL,
@ha_volt varchar(250)=NULL,
@generation_involved varchar(250)=NULL,
@cust_ref varchar(250),
@type_of_services varchar(250)=NULL,
@no_end_connection varchar(250)=NULL,
@site_name varchar(250)=NULL,
@free_text varchar(250)=NULL,
@sub_street varchar(250)=NULL,
@building_name varchar(250)=NULL,
@subbuilding_name varchar(250)=NULL,
@dd_locality varchar(250)=NULL,
@street varchar(250)=NULL,
@d_locality varchar(250)=NULL,
@town varchar(250)=NULL,
@county varchar(250)=NULL,
@post_code varchar(250)=NULL,
@easting varchar(25)=NULL,
@northing varchar(25)=NULL,
@contid varchar(10),
@addrid varchar(10),
--@AE_AEID varchar(250),
--@ae_address varchar(250),
@internal_referance varchar(250) out)
AS 

IF @cust_ref IS NULL
BEGIN
SET @internal_referance='Issue'
END
ELSE 
BEGIN
DECLARE @AE_AEID int,
@ae_address varchar(250),
@CUST_ID varchar(250);

	SET @ae_address = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ADDRESS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/

	INSERT INTO BT_ADDRESS_MASTER 
		(ADDRESS_ID,
		 parentID,
		 siteName,
		 addressLine1,
		 addressLine2,
		 addressLine3,
		 addressLine4,
		 addressLine5,
		 addressLine6,
		 addressLine7,
		 addressLine8,
		 addressLine9,
		 postCode,dateCreated,lastUpdated,easting,Northing,createdBy,lastUpdatedBy,addressUsage)
	VALUES
		(@ae_address,
		(SELECT CUST_ID FROM BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER WHERE CUST_REFERENCE=@cust_ref),
		@site_name,
		@free_text,
		@subbuilding_name,
		@building_name,
		@sub_street,
		@street,
		@dd_locality,
		@d_locality,
		@town,
		@county,
		@post_code,
		GETDATE(),
		GETDATE(),
		@easting,
		@northing,
		@ploggedonuser,
		@ploggedonuser,2);

	SET @AE_AEID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/

	
	SET @internal_referance=CONCAT('ENQ',@AE_AEID) ;

	INSERT INTO BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER 
		( AE_AEID,ae_address,AE_internalRef,AE_externalRef,AE_dateOrigin,AE_orderReceivedDate,AE_summaryDescription,AE_assignedToDept,AE_assignedToPerson,
		  AE_status,AE_type,AE_plotUnit,AE_networkDistributor,AE_metaModelType,AE_metaModelType2,AE_MPANRequested,AE_isTemplate,AE_sendToCFS,AE_customer,AE_createdBy,
		  AE_dateCreated,AE_lastUpdatedBy,AE_lastUpdated,AE_contact,AE_CustomerAddress,AE_companyID,AE_deleted,AE_taskInstruction,AE_useUplift,AE_Category,AE_stdCode)
	 VALUES
		(@AE_AEID,@ae_address,@internal_referance,@external_referance,getdate(),getdate(),REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(@enquiry_description, CHAR(13),' '), CHAR(10),' '),'&',' '),@assinged_to_dept,@assinged_to_person,
		 @status,@source,@plot,@network_distributor,'T',1,'N','N','N',(SELECT CUST_ID FROM BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER WHERE CUST_REFERENCE=@cust_ref),@ploggedonuser,
		 GETDATE(),@ploggedonuser,getdate(),@contid,@addrid,1000,'N','Y','Y',-1,'NONE');

	INSERT INTO MT_ENQUIRY_QUESTIONS  
		(aeq_ae_aeid,aeq_POC_Voltage_LOV,aeq_HA_Voltage_LOV,aeq_GENERATION_LOV,aeq_TOW_REQUIRED_LOV,aeq_NUM_End_Connection_LOV,aeq_create_date,aeq_created_by,aeq_updated_by,aeq_updated_date) 
	VALUES
		(@AE_AEID,@poc_volt,@ha_volt,@generation_involved,@type_of_services,@no_end_connection,GETDATE(),@ploggedonuser,@ploggedonuser,GETDATE());

		EXEC sp_ACT_addHIST_addEnquiry @AE_AEID, @ploggedonuser, @assinged_to_dept, @assinged_to_person, @status, ''
END
GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_ADD_ENQUIRY_V2]  
(@ploggedonuser INT,  
@external_referance varchar(50)=NULL,  
@npg_ref varchar(50)=NULL,  
@enquiry_description varchar(2000)=NULL,  
@assinged_to_dept varchar(25)=NULL,  
@assinged_to_person varchar(25)=NULL,  
@status varchar(25)=NULL,  
@source varchar(25)=NULL,  
@plot varchar(50)=NULL,  
@network_distributor varchar(25)=NULL,  
@poc_volt varchar(25)=NULL,  
@ha_volt varchar(25)=NULL,  
@generation_involved varchar(25)=NULL,  
@cust_ref varchar(25),  
@type_of_services varchar(25)=NULL,  
@no_end_connection varchar(25)=NULL,  
@site_name varchar(250)=NULL,  
@free_text varchar(50)=NULL,  
@sub_street varchar(50)=NULL,  
@building_name varchar(50)=NULL,  
@subbuilding_name varchar(50)=NULL,  
@dd_locality varchar(50)=NULL,  
@street varchar(50)=NULL,  
@d_locality varchar(50)=NULL,  
@town varchar(50)=NULL,  
@county varchar(50)=NULL,  
@post_code varchar(50)=NULL,  
@easting varchar(10)=NULL,  
@northing varchar(10)=NULL,  
@contid varchar(10),  
@addrid varchar(10),  
@cust_type varchar(200)=NULL,  
--@AE_AEID varchar(250),  
--@ae_address varchar(250),  
@appType varchar(20)=NULL,  
@AE_QuoteProjectDiscuss varchar(50)=NULL,  
@AE_QuoteProvision varchar(50)=NULL,  
@internal_referance varchar(20) out,  
@AE_CompReciveDate varchar(100)= NULL,  
@AE_AppReciveDate varchar(100)= NULL,  
@SearchedPostcode varchar(100)= NULL,  
@quoteDesigner varchar(25)=NULL,

@AE_OWNER_OCCUPIER_DETAILS varchar(5)=NULL,    
@AE_LETTER_OF_AUTHORITY  varchar(5)=NULL,    
@AE_ADDRESS_OF_PREMISES  varchar(5)=NULL,    
@AE_TOTAL_LOAD  varchar(5)=NULL,    
@AE_DATE_OF_CONNECTION  varchar(5)=NULL,    
@AE_ProgrammeStartEnddate varchar(5)=NULL,    
@AE_Siteplanindicatingsiteboundary varchar(5)=NULL,    
@AE_TechnicalDetailsGeneration varchar(5)=NULL,    
@AE_TechnicalDetailsHeatpump varchar(5)=NULL,    
@AE_MeteringPoints varchar(5)=NULL,     
@AE_TechnicalDetailsMotor varchar(5)=NULL,    
@AE_TechnicalDetailsElectricHeating varchar(5)=NULL,    
@AE_TechnicalDetailsEVChargingPoint varchar(5)=NULL,    
@AE_Lengthoftimerequired varchar(5)=NULL,    
@AE_TechnicalDetailsWelder varchar(5)=NULL,    
@AE_PlanningPermission varchar(5)=NULL,    
@AE_ApplicantDetails varchar(5)=NULL,    
@connectionRequiredDate varchar(25) = NULL,
@AE_Substation varchar(5)=NULL,
@WorkType varchar(50) = NULL,
@AE_HeadOfMinimumInformation varchar(5)=NULL, 
@AE_PreliminaryProjectTimeLine varchar(5)=NULL,
@AE_PaymentRequired varchar(50) = NULL,
@AE_DetailedEngineeringDesign varchar(50) = NULL,
@AE_MaximumCapacitykVA varchar(50) = NULL,
@AE_SingleLineDiagram varchar(50) = NULL,
@AE_Siteplanboundary varchar(50) = NULL,
@AE_ServiceRequired varchar(50) = NULL
)  
  
AS   
  
  
DECLARE @own_Line1 varchar(50),  
@own_Line2 varchar(50),  
@own_Line3 varchar(50),  
@own_Line4 varchar(50),  
@own_Line5 varchar(50),  
@own_Line6 varchar(50),  
@own_Line7 varchar(50),  
@own_Line8 varchar(50),  
@own_Line9 varchar(50),  
@own_PostCode varchar(50),  
@own_Easting varchar(50),  
@own_Northing varchar(50),  
@alt_Line1 varchar(50),  
@alt_Line2 varchar(50),  
@alt_Line3 varchar(50),  
@alt_Line4 varchar(50),  
@alt_Line5 varchar(50),  
@alt_Line6 varchar(50),  
@alt_Line7 varchar(50),  
@alt_Line8 varchar(50),  
@alt_Line9 varchar(50),  
@alt_PostCode varchar(50),  
@alt_Easting varchar(50),  
@alt_Northing varchar(50),  
@AppReciveDateDt datetime,  
@AppRecieveString varchar(19),

@Enq_Id int  ;  
  
  
  
IF @cust_ref IS NULL  
BEGIN  
SET @internal_referance='Issue'  
END  
ELSE   
BEGIN  
DECLARE @AE_AEID int,  
@ae_address varchar(250),  
@CUST_ID varchar(250);  
  
 SET @ae_address = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ADDRESS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/  
  
 INSERT INTO BT_ADDRESS_MASTER   
  (ADDRESS_ID,  
   parentID,  
   siteName,  
   addressLine1,  
   addressLine2,  
   addressLine3,  
   addressLine4,  
   addressLine5,  
   addressLine6,  
   addressLine7,  
   addressLine8,  
   addressLine9,  
   postCode,dateCreated,lastUpdated,easting,Northing,createdBy,lastUpdatedBy,addressUsage)  
 VALUES  
  (@ae_address,  
  (SELECT CUST_ID FROM BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER WHERE CUST_REFERENCE=@cust_ref),  
  CONCAT(@building_name,', ',@street,', ',@d_locality,', ',@town,', ',@post_code),--@site_name,  
  @free_text,  
  @subbuilding_name,  
  @building_name,  
  @sub_street,  
  @street,  
  @dd_locality,  
  @d_locality,  
  @town,  
  @county,  
  @post_code,  
  GETDATE(),  
  GETDATE(),  
  @easting,  
  @northing,  
  @ploggedonuser,  
  @ploggedonuser,2);  
  
  
 UPDATE BT_ADDRESS_MASTER SET   
 sitename =dbo.fn_rpt_GetReformatReturnAddress_NoSiteName(@ae_address)  
 where ADDRESS_ID=@ae_address;  
  
 SET @AE_AEID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/  
  
   
 SET @internal_referance=CONCAT('ENQ',@AE_AEID) ;  
  
 INSERT INTO BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER   
  ( AE_AEID,ae_address,AE_internalRef,AE_externalRef,AE_dateOrigin,AE_orderReceivedDate,AE_summaryDescription,AE_assignedToDept,AE_assignedToPerson,  
    AE_status,AE_type,AE_plotUnit,AE_networkDistributor,AE_metaModelType,AE_metaModelType2,AE_MPANRequested,AE_isTemplate,AE_sendToCFS,AE_customer,AE_createdBy,  
    AE_dateCreated,AE_lastUpdatedBy,AE_lastUpdated,AE_contact,AE_CustomerAddress,AE_companyID,AE_deleted,AE_taskInstruction,AE_useUplift,AE_Category,AE_stdCode,  
    AE_externalRef2  
    ,AE_CustType,AE_externalRef3,AE_QuoteProjectDiscuss,AE_QuoteProvision,AE_AppReciveDate,AE_CompReciveDate,AE_quoteDesigner, AE_WorkType  
    )  
  VALUES  
  (@AE_AEID,@ae_address,@internal_referance,substring(@external_referance,1,50),CONVERT(smalldatetime,@AE_CompReciveDate),CONVERT(smalldatetime,@AE_AppReciveDate),REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(@enquiry_description, CHAR(13),' '), CHAR(10),' '),'&',' '),@assinged_to_dept,@assinged_to_person,  
   @status,@source,@plot,@network_distributor,'T',1,'N','N','N',(SELECT CUST_ID FROM BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER WHERE CUST_REFERENCE=@cust_ref),@ploggedonuser,  
   GETDATE(),@ploggedonuser,getdate(),@contid,@addrid,1000,'N','Y','Y',-1,'NONE',@npg_ref  
   ,isnull(@cust_type,0),isnull(@appType,0),isnull(@AE_QuoteProjectDiscuss,0),isnull(@AE_QuoteProvision,0),@AE_AppReciveDate,@AE_CompReciveDate,@quoteDesigner, @WorkType  
   );  

  --set @Enq_Id=SCOPE_IDENTITY();  
SET @Enq_Id = @AE_AEID; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/ 

 INSERT INTO MT_ENQUIRY_QUESTIONS    
  (aeq_ae_aeid,aeq_POC_Voltage_LOV,aeq_HA_Voltage_LOV,aeq_GENERATION_LOV,aeq_TOW_REQUIRED_LOV,aeq_NUM_End_Connection_LOV,aeq_create_date,aeq_created_by,aeq_updated_by,aeq_updated_date,SearchedPostcode)   
 VALUES  
  (@AE_AEID,@poc_volt,@ha_volt,@generation_involved,@type_of_services,@no_end_connection,GETDATE(),@ploggedonuser,@ploggedonuser,GETDATE(),@SearchedPostcode);  
  
  
  IF @appType='Owner/Occupier'  
  BEGIN  
  SELECT @own_Line1=addressLine1 ,@own_Line2=addressLine2 ,@own_Line3 =addressLine3 ,@own_Line4 =addressLine4  
 ,@own_Line5=addressLine5 ,@own_Line6=addressLine6 ,@own_Line7 =addressLine7,@own_Line8=addressLine8   
 ,@own_Line9=addressLine9 ,@own_PostCode=postCode,@own_Easting=easting,@own_Northing=@northing,  
  @alt_Line1=NULL,@alt_Line2=NULL,@alt_Line3=NULL,@alt_Line4=NULL,@alt_Line5=NULL,@alt_Line6=NULL,@alt_Line7=NULL  
 ,@alt_Line8=NULL,@alt_Line9=NULL,@alt_PostCode=NULL,@alt_Easting=NULL,@alt_Northing=NULL  
 FROM BT_ADDRESS_MASTER where Address_id=@addrid  
  END  
  ELSE IF @appType='Agent'  
  BEGIN  
  SELECT @own_Line1=NULL ,@own_Line2=NULL ,@own_Line3 =NULL ,@own_Line4 =NULL  
 ,@own_Line5=NULL ,@own_Line6= NULL,@own_Line7 =NULL,@own_Line8=NULL   
 ,@own_Line9=NULL ,@own_PostCode=NULL,@own_Easting=NULL,@own_Northing=NULL,  
  @alt_Line1=addressLine1,@alt_Line2=addressLine2,@alt_Line3=addressLine3,@alt_Line4=addressLine4,  
  @alt_Line5=addressLine5,@alt_Line6=addressLine6,@alt_Line7=addressLine7  
 ,@alt_Line8=addressLine8,@alt_Line9=addressLine9,@alt_PostCode=postCode,@alt_Easting=easting,@alt_Northing=Northing  
 FROM BT_ADDRESS_MASTER where Address_id=@addrid  
  END  
 EXEC [PRC_ACT_editContactDetails] @AE_AEID,0,NULL,@own_Line1 ,@own_Line2 ,@own_Line3 ,@own_Line4   
 ,@own_Line5 ,@own_Line6 ,@own_Line7 ,@own_Line8 ,@own_Line9 ,@own_PostCode,NULL,@own_Easting,@own_Northing,  
 0,NULL, @alt_Line1,@alt_Line2,@alt_Line3,@alt_Line4,@alt_Line5,@alt_Line6,@alt_Line7,@alt_Line8,@alt_Line9,  
 @alt_PostCode,NULL,NULL,NULL,@alt_Easting,@alt_Northing,@ploggedonuser,NULL,NULL,NULL,NULL   
  
 set @AppRecieveString = concat(SUBSTRING(@AE_AppReciveDate,1,11),convert(varchar, getdate(),8))  
 set @AppReciveDateDt = (select convert(datetime, @AppRecieveString ,120))  
  
 EXEC[SP_ADD_STANDARDS]  @ploggedonuser,@internal_referance, 294, @AppReciveDateDt, 5000,1  
   
  EXEC sp_ACT_addHIST_addEnquiry @AE_AEID, @ploggedonuser, @assinged_to_dept, @assinged_to_person, @status, ''  

  INSERT INTO [dbo].[BT_ENQUIRY_MINIMUM_INFORMATION]  
   ( AE_ENQ_ID,  
   AE_OWNER_OCCUPIER_DETAILS  ,  
AE_LETTER_OF_AUTHORITY  ,  
AE_ADDRESS_OF_PREMISES   ,  
AE_TOTAL_LOAD ,  
AE_DATE_OF_CONNECTION  ,  
AE_CREATED_BY ,  
AE_CREATED_ON  ,  
AE_UPDATED_BY  ,  
AE_UPDATED_ON ,  
AE_ProgrammeStartEnddate ,  
AE_Siteplanindicatingsiteboundary ,  
AE_TechnicalDetailsGeneration ,  
AE_TechnicalDetailsHeatpump ,  
AE_MeteringPoints ,  
AE_TechnicalDetailsMotor ,  
AE_TechnicalDetailsElectricHeating ,  
AE_TechnicalDetailsEVChargingPoint ,  
AE_Lengthoftimerequired ,  
AE_TechnicalDetailsWelder ,  
AE_PlanningPermission ,  
AE_ApplicantDetails,
AE_Substation,
AE_HeadOfMinimumInformation,
AE_PreliminaryProjectTimeLine,
AE_PaymentRequired,
AE_DetailedEngineeringDesign,
AE_MaximumCapacitykVA,
AE_SingleLineDiagram,
AE_Siteplanboundary,
AE_ServiceRequired) 
  VALUES 
  (
  @Enq_Id ,  
@AE_OWNER_OCCUPIER_DETAILS ,  
@AE_LETTER_OF_AUTHORITY  ,  
@AE_ADDRESS_OF_PREMISES  ,  
@AE_TOTAL_LOAD  ,  
@AE_DATE_OF_CONNECTION  ,  
@ploggedonuser,  
GETDATE(),  
@ploggedonuser,  
GETDATE() ,  
@AE_ProgrammeStartEnddate,  
@AE_Siteplanindicatingsiteboundary,  
@AE_TechnicalDetailsGeneration,  
@AE_TechnicalDetailsHeatpump,

)

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_ADD_SITE_ADDRESS]
@ae_customer int,
@addr_siteName varchar(250),
@addr_Line1 varchar(255),
@addr_Line2 varchar(255),
@addr_Line3 varchar(255),
@addr_Line4 varchar(255),
@addr_Line5 varchar(255),
@addr_Line6 varchar(255),
@addr_Line7 varchar(255),
@addr_Line8 varchar(255),
@addr_Line9 varchar(255),
@addr_PostCode varchar(255),
@addr_Northing varchar(255),
@addr_Easting varchar(255),
@addr_PAFKey varchar(255),


@newID int OUTPUT,
@userID int
 
AS
--print '@ae_customer' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),coalesce(@ae_customer,''));
DECLARE @V410_AddrId int; SET @V410_AddrId = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ADDRESS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/
INSERT BT_ADDRESS_MASTER(
	ADDRESS_ID,
	parentID,
	siteName,
	addressLine1,
	addressLine2,
	addressLine3,
	addressLine4,
	addressLine5,
	addressLine6,
	addressLine7,
	addressLine8,
	addressLine9,
	postCode,
	Northing,
	Easting ,
	dateCreated,
	createdBy,
	lastUpdated,
	lastUpdatedBy,
	deleted,
	addressUsage
 )
VALUES(
@V410_AddrId /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/,
@ae_customer,
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_siteName)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line1)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line2)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line3)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line4)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line5)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line6)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line7)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line8)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Line9)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_PostCode)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Northing)),
	LTRIM(RTRIM(@addr_Easting)),
	getDate(),
	@userID,
	getDate(),
	@userID,
	'N',
	2
)
SET @newID = @V410_AddrId; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/ 

GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_UPDATE_CUST_DTLS_BY_CUSTREF]

	@ploggedonUser				VARCHAR(25),
	@pTelephone 				VARCHAR(50)= NULL,
	@pCFSID 					VARCHAR(50)= NULL,
	@pCustName					VARCHAR(50)= NULL,
	@pEmail						VARCHAR(50)= NULL,
	@pCustType					VARCHAR(20)= NULL,
	@pPrimaryAddress			int= NULL,
	@pPrimaryContact			int= NULL,
	@pCustRef					VARCHAR(25),
	@pCustRelationshipManager	int = NULL,
	@pErrorMessage				VARCHAR(5000) OUTPUT 

/**************************************************************											
--	Procedure Name	:	@uspUpdateCustomerDetails
--	Description		:	Update Customer Details
--	Parameters		:	
						
						@pErrorMessage - Output parameter to return the error message

--	Return Value	:	0	Success			
--						1	Failure

--	Date Modified	Modified By				Modifications
--	15-March-2017		Madhumita					
**************************************************************/
AS
BEGIN

	SET NOCOUNT ON;

BEGIN TRY
		SET @pErrorMessage = ''
		DECLARE @vCustOrgName VARCHAR(50)
		DECLARE @vPrimAddId INT
		DECLARE @vContactID INT
		DECLARE @vcount INT
	--	SELECT @vPrimConId = CUST_PRIM_CONTACTID from BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER
	--	SELECT @vPrimAddId = CUST_PRIM_ADDRID from BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER
	select @vCustOrgName=cust_org_name from BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER where CUST_REFERENCE	=	@pCustRef
	select @vcount=count(*) from BT_CONTACTS_MASTER where con_parentID =(select CUST_ID from BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER where CUST_REFERENCE=@pCustRef )
		and con_forename=@vCustOrgName
	select @vContactID=con_contactID from BT_CONTACTS_MASTER where con_parentID =(select CUST_ID from BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER where CUST_REFERENCE=@pCustRef )
		and con_forename=@vCustOrgName

	if(@pCustType='Domestic')
	BEGIN
		UPDATE BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER SET
				CUST_ORG_NAME = @pCustName,
				CUST_TYPE = 'Domestic',
				CUST_TELEPHONE	=	@pTelephone,
				CFSID			=	@pCFSID,
				CUST_PRIM_CONTACTID			=	@pPrimaryContact,
				CUST_PRIM_ADDRID			=	@pPrimaryAddress,
				UPDATED_ON		=	GETDATE(),
				UPDATED_BY		=	@ploggedonUser,
				custRelationshipManager = @pCustRelationshipManager
		WHERE	CUST_REFERENCE	=	@pCustRef

		
	END
	if(@pCustType='Commercial')
	BEGIN
		UPDATE BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER SET
				CUST_ORG_NAME = @pCustName,
				CUST_TYPE = 'Commercial',
				CUST_TELEPHONE	=	@pTelephone,
				CFSID			=	@pCFSID,
				CUST_PRIM_CONTACTID			=	@pPrimaryContact,
				CUST_PRIM_ADDRID			=	@pPrimaryAddress,
				UPDATED_ON		=	GETDATE(),
				UPDATED_BY		=	@ploggedonUser,
				custRelationshipManager = @pCustRelationshipManager
		WHERE	CUST_REFERENCE	=	@pCustRef

		
	END
	if(@pCustType='ICP')
	BEGIN
		UPDATE BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER SET
				CUST_ORG_NAME = @pCustName,
				CUST_TYPE = 'ICP',
				CUST_TELEPHONE	=	@pTelephone,
				CFSID			=	@pCFSID,
				CUST_PRIM_CONTACTID			=	@pPrimaryContact,
				CUST_PRIM_ADDRID			=	@pPrimaryAddress,
				UPDATED_ON		=	GETDATE(),
				UPDATED_BY		=	@ploggedonUser,
				custRelationshipManager = @pCustRelationshipManager
		WHERE	CUST_REFERENCE	=	@pCustRef

		
	END
	IF @vcount = 0 
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @V410_ConId int; SET @V410_ConId = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_CONTACTS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/
		INSERT INTO BT_CONTACTS_MASTER
				   ([con_contactID],
				    [con_parentID],
				    [con_parentType],[con_surname] ,[con_forename]
				   ,[con_personalTitle],[con_jobTitle] ,[con_telephone],
				    [con_email] --,[con_mobile]
					 ,[con_dateCreated],[con_createdBy],[con_lastUpdated],
				   [con_lastUpdatedBy],[con_deleted],[con_CFSID]
				   )
					
					SELECT 
					@V410_ConId /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/,
					(SELECT cust_id from BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER where CUST_REFERENCE = @pCustRef),
					1,'',@pCustName,'NA','',@pTelephone,@pEmail,
					--@pMobile,
					GETDATE(),
					@ploggedonUser,GETDATE(),@ploggedonUser,'N',@pCFSID 
	END 
	ELSE
	 BEGIN
	  UPDATE [dbo].BT_CONTACTS_MASTER
		SET	     [con_forename] = @pCustName
		        ,[con_telephone] = @pTelephone
			  	,[con_email] = @pEmail
				,[con_CFSID] = @pCFSID
				--,[con_mobile] = @pMobile
				,[con_lastUpdated] = GETDATE()
				,[con_lastUpdatedBy] = @ploggedonUser
		WHERE   con_contactID=@vContactID
		-- con_parentID =(select CUST_ID from BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER where CUST_REFERENCE=@pCustRef )
		--and con_forename=(select CUST_ORG_NAME from BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER where CUST_REFERENCE=@pCustRef  )
	 END
		
/*
			UPDATE BT_ADDRESS_MASTER SET
				addressLine1	 = @pPrimaryAddress,
				lastUpdated		=	GETDATE(),
				lastUpdatedBy	=	@ploggedonUser
		WHERE	ADDRESS_ID		=	@vPrimAddId

		UPDATE BT_CONTACTS_MASTER SET
				con_telephone		= @pPrimaryContact,
				con_lastUpdated		=	GETDATE(),
				con_lastUpdatedBy	=	@ploggedonUser
		WHERE	con_contactID		=	@vPrimConId*/

																	

			

		RETURN 0
END TRY

BEGIN CATCH
		SELECT @pErrorMessage='Error Code: ' + CAST (ERROR_NUMBER() AS VARCHAR(10)) + ', Error: ' + ERROR_MESSAGE()
		RETURN 1
END CATCH	
END 





GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON

-- =============================================
-- Author:		TCS
-- Create date: 28-Nov-2019
-- Description:	Add Enquiry
-- =============================================
ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_addEnquiry]
(@ploggedonuser INT,
@external_referance varchar(250)=NULL,
@enquiry_description varchar(250)=NULL,
@assinged_to_dept varchar(250)=NULL,
@assinged_to_person varchar(250)=NULL,
@status varchar(250)=NULL,
@source varchar(250)=NULL,
@plot varchar(250)=NULL,
@network_distributor varchar(250)=NULL,
@poc_volt varchar(250)=NULL,
@ha_volt varchar(250)=NULL,
@generation_involved varchar(250)=NULL,
@cust_ref varchar(250),
@type_of_services varchar(250)=NULL,
@no_end_connection varchar(250)=NULL,
@site_name varchar(250)=NULL,
@free_text varchar(250)=NULL,
@sub_street varchar(250)=NULL,
@building_name varchar(250)=NULL,
@dd_locality varchar(250)=NULL,
@street varchar(250)=NULL,
@d_locality varchar(250)=NULL,
@town varchar(250)=NULL,
@county varchar(250)=NULL,
@post_code varchar(250)=NULL,
@easting varchar(25)=NULL,
@northing varchar(25)=NULL,
@contid varchar(10),
@addrid varchar(10),
--@AE_AEID varchar(250),
--@ae_address varchar(250),
@internal_referance varchar(250) out)
AS 


DECLARE @AE_AEID int,
@ae_address varchar(250),
@CUST_ID varchar(250);

	SET @ae_address = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ADDRESS_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/

	INSERT INTO BT_ADDRESS_MASTER 
		(ADDRESS_ID,parentID,siteName,addressLine1,addressLine2,addressLine3,
		 addressLine4,addressLine5,addressLine7,addressLine8,addressLine9, postCode,dateCreated,lastUpdated,easting,Northing,createdBy,lastUpdatedBy)
	VALUES
		(@ae_address,(SELECT CUST_ID FROM BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER WHERE CUST_REFERENCE=@cust_ref),@site_name,@free_text,@sub_street,@building_name,@dd_locality,@street,@d_locality,@town,@county,@post_code,GETDATE(),GETDATE(),@easting,@northing,@ploggedonuser,@ploggedonuser);

	SET @AE_AEID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/

	
	SET @internal_referance=CONCAT('ENQ',@AE_AEID) ;

	INSERT INTO BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER 
		( AE_AEID,ae_address,AE_internalRef,AE_externalRef,AE_dateOrigin,AE_orderReceivedDate,AE_summaryDescription,AE_assignedToDept,AE_assignedToPerson,
		  AE_status,AE_type,AE_plotUnit,AE_networkDistributor,AE_metaModelType,AE_metaModelType2,AE_MPANRequested,AE_isTemplate,AE_sendToCFS,AE_customer,AE_createdBy,
		  AE_dateCreated,AE_lastUpdatedBy,AE_lastUpdated,AE_contact,AE_CustomerAddress,AE_companyID,AE_deleted,AE_taskInstruction,AE_useUplift)
	 VALUES
		(@AE_AEID,@addrid,@internal_referance,@external_referance,getdate(),getdate(),@enquiry_description,@assinged_to_dept,@assinged_to_person,
		 @status,@source,@plot,@network_distributor,'T',1,'N','N','N',(SELECT CUST_ID FROM BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER WHERE CUST_REFERENCE=@cust_ref),@ploggedonuser,
		 GETDATE(),@ploggedonuser,getdate(),@contid,@addrid,1000,'N','Y','Y');

	INSERT INTO MT_ENQUIRY_QUESTIONS  
		(aeq_id,aeq_ae_aeid,aeq_POC_Voltage_LOV,aeq_HA_Voltage_LOV,aeq_GENERATION_LOV,aeq_TOW_REQUIRED_LOV,aeq_NUM_End_Connection_LOV,aeq_create_date,aeq_created_by,aeq_updated_by,aeq_updated_date) 
	VALUES
		((SELECT MAX(aeq_id)+1 FROM MT_ENQUIRY_QUESTIONS),@AE_AEID,@poc_volt,@ha_volt,@generation_involved,@type_of_services,@no_end_connection,GETDATE(),@ploggedonuser,@ploggedonuser,GETDATE());


		EXEC sp_ACT_addHIST_addEnquiry @AE_AEID, @ploggedonuser, @assinged_to_dept, @assinged_to_person, @status, ''
GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

 WHERE ADDRESS_ID=@Corres_AddID

-- CTK Insertion
	IF @parentid > 10000000 AND @parentid<25000000
		BEGIN
		 select @NewAE_AEID = CTK_SEQ from MT_SEQ_MANAGER_CEES
