ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_ADD_BOM_QMS] 
	@iBOM_estimateParentRef varchar(20),
	@iBOM_investmentDriver varchar(50), 
	@iBOM_name varchar(100), 
	@iBOM_type varchar(50), 
	@iBOM_priceBook varchar(50), 
	@iBOM_taskType varchar(50), 
	@iBOM_description varchar(2000),
	@iBOM_status varchar(50), 
	@iBOM_addToProject int, 
	@iBOM_dummyProject varchar(1), 
	@iBOM_customerContributionPercent float,
	@iBOM_uplift  float=0,--changes for uplift
	@userid int,
	@newBOMID varchar(50) OUTPUT
	 --,@DateCompleted smallDateTime=NULL
AS

DECLARE 	@vBOMID INT,
	@vBOMInternalRef VARCHAR(20),
	@iBOM_estimateParentID VARCHAR(20),
	@AE_assignedtodept int,
	@AE_designer int;
	
	SELECT @iBOM_estimateParentID=AE_AEID FROM BT_ESTIMATE_MASTER WHERE AE_internalRef=@iBOM_estimateParentRef;
	--SELECT @AE_designer=AE_designer, @AE_assignedtodept=AE_assignedToDept from BT_QUOTE_MASTER WHERE AE_AEID=@iBOM_estimateParentID
	SELECT @AE_designer=q.AE_designer, @AE_assignedtodept=q.AE_assignedToDept from BT_QUOTE_MASTER q,BT_ESTIMATE_MASTER e WHERE q.AE_AEID=e.AE_ParentID and e.AE_AEID=@iBOM_estimateParentID
	
	SET @vBOMID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/

INSERT INTO BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS
(	
	BOM_bomID,
	BOM_internalRef,
	BOM_estimateParentID, 
	BOM_investmentDriver, 
	BOM_name, 
	BOM_type, 

	BOM_priceBook, 
	BOM_taskType, 
	BOM_description, 

	BOM_openDate,  
	BOM_generatedProject, 

	BOM_addToProject, 
	BOM_dummyProject, 
	BOM_customerContributionPercent, 

	BOM_createdBy, 
	BOM_dateCreated, 
	BOM_lastUpdatedBy, 
	BOM_lastUpdated, 
	BOM_deleted,
	BOM_assignedToDesigner,
	BOM_assignedToDept,
	BOM_status,
	BOM_taskCategoryTotalCost,
	BOM_addUpliftPercent,
	BOM_deliveryProjectCost,
	BOM_addedUpliftMoney,BOM_customerContributionMoney,BOM_lockTaskCategories,BOM_addedUpliftDeliveryCost,BOM_applyMargin
	--,BOM_completeDate
	--BOM_Uplift--changes for uplift
)
VALUES
(
	@vBOMID,
	CONCAT('BOM', @vBOMID),
	@iBOM_estimateParentID, 
	@iBOM_investmentDriver, 
	@iBOM_name, 
	@iBOM_type, 

	@iBOM_priceBook, 
	@iBOM_taskType, 
	@iBOM_description, 

	getDate(), 
	null, 

	@iBOM_addToProject, 
	@iBOM_dummyProject,--'Y',-- 
	@iBOM_customerContributionPercent, 

	@userid, 
	getDate(), 
	@userID, 
	getDate(), 
	'N',
	
	@AE_designer,
	@AE_assignedtodept,
	1,
	0,0,0,0,0,'N',0,'Y'
	--,@DateCompleted
	--@iBOM_uplift--changes for uplift
	);

	INSERT INTO BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY
	 ([TC_TCID]
      ,[TC_parentBOMID]
      ,[TC_seq]
      ,[TC_priceBook]
      ,[TC_name]
      ,[TC_description]
      ,[TC_exclProj]
      ,[TC_totalCost]
      ,[TC_template]
      ,[TC_sourceTemplateTCID]
      ,[TC_dateCreated]
      ,[TC_createdBy]
      ,[TC_lastUpdated]
      ,[TC_lastUpdatedBy]
      ,[TC_deleted]
      ,[TC_totalCost_Oncost])

  SELECT  NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY OVER (ORDER BY TC_seq) /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/ as tcid,
	   @vBOMID
      ,[TC_seq]
      ,[TC_priceBook]
      ,[TC_name]
      ,[TC_description]
      ,[TC_exclProj]
      ,[TC_totalCost]
      ,'N'
      ,[TC_TCID],        
		getDate(), 
		@userID, 
		getDate(),
		@userid,
       [TC_deleted]
      ,[TC_totalCost_Oncost]
  FROM MT_TASKCATEGORY where TC_priceBook=@iBOM_priceBook /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/;	


  INSERT INTO [BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS](--[TCI_ID]
     -- ,
	  [TCI_parentTCID]
      ,[TCI_priceBook]
      ,[TCI_type]
      ,[TCI_code]
      ,[TCI_sequence]
      ,[TCI_itemName]
      ,[TCI_materialsCost]
      ,[TCI_labourCost]
      ,[TCI_contractCost]
      ,[TCI_contestSplit]
      ,[TCI_nonContestSplit]
      ,[TCI_labourHours]
      ,[TCI_contractZone]
      ,[TCI_displayContest]
      ,[TCI_displayNonContest]
      ,[TCI_displayDesc]
      ,[TCI_contestValueMoney]
      ,[TCI_contestValueMoneyDefault]
      ,[TCI_contestValQty]
      ,[TCI_contestValQtyDefault]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValMoney]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValMoneyDefault]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValQty]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValQtyDefault]
      ,[TCI_description]
      ,[TCI_totalCost]
      ,[TCI_lovName]
      ,[TCI_itemToUpdate]
      ,[TCI_isTemplate]
      ,[TCI_selectedLOVValue]
      ,[TCI_sourceTemplateTCI_ID]
      ,[TCI_dateCreated]
      ,[TCI_createdBy]
      ,[TCI_lastUpdated]
      ,[TCI_lastUpdatedBy]
      ,[TCI_deleted]
      ,[TCI_calculatedTotalLabourHour]
      ,[TCI_labourApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_materialsApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_contractApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_totalCost_Oncost])

SELECT --(SELECT MAX(TCI_ID) +1 FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS)+ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY TCI_ID)
	  --,
	  (SELECT TC_TCID FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY M WHERE TC_parentBOMID=@vBOMID and M.TC_sourceTemplateTCID=tasks.TC_sourceTemplateTCID )
      ,[TCI_priceBook]
      ,[TCI_type]
      ,[TCI_code]
      ,asg.[TCAI_seq]
      ,[TCI_itemName]
      ,[TCI_materialsCost]
      ,[TCI_labourCost]
      ,[TCI_contractCost]
      ,[TCI_contestSplit]
      ,[TCI_nonContestSplit]
      ,[TCI_labourHours]
      ,[TCI_contractZone]
      ,[TCI_displayContest]
      ,[TCI_displayNonContest]
      ,[TCI_displayDesc]
      ,[TCI_contestValueMoney]
      ,[TCI_contestValueMoneyDefault]
      ,[TCI_contestValQty]
      ,[TCI_contestValQtyDefault]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValMoney]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValMoneyDefault]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValQty]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValQtyDefault]
      ,[TCI_description]
      ,[TCI_totalCost]
      ,[TCI_lovName]
      ,[TCI_itemToUpdate]
      ,'N'
      ,[TCI_selectedLOVValue]
      ,[TCI_sourceTemplateTCI_ID]
	 
	  ,getDate() 
	 ,@userID 
	  ,getDate() 
      ,@userid 
	  ,[TCI_deleted]
      ,[TCI_calculatedTotalLabourHour]
      ,[TCI_labourApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_materialsApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_contractApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_totalCost_Oncost]
      
  FROM [MT_PRICEBOOK] PRICEBOOK,BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYASSIGNNEDITEMS asg,
   (SELECT * FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY tasks WHERE TC_parentBOMID=@vBOMID  and tasks.TC_priceBook=@iBOM_priceBook
 ) TASKS
  WHERE PRICEBOOK.TCI_ID=asg.TCAI_ITEMID  
  AND asg.TCAI_TASKCATEGORYID = tasks.TC_sourceTemplateTCID 
 AND asg.TCAI_deleted = 'N'   
 order by  [TCI_sequence];


	--SELECT CONCAT('BOM', @vBOMID);
	SET @newBOMID=CONCAT('BOM', @vBOMID);

GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_UPDATE_BOM] 
	@iBOM_estimateParentRef varchar(20),
	@iBOM_investmentDriver varchar(50), 
	@iBOM_name varchar(100), 
	@iBOM_type varchar(50), 
	@iBOM_priceBook varchar(50), 
	@iBOM_taskType varchar(50), 
	@iBOM_description varchar(2000),
	@iBOM_status varchar(50), 
	@iBOM_addToProject int, 
	@iBOM_dummyProject varchar(1), 
	@iBOM_customerContributionPercent float, 
	@userid int,
	@newBOMID varchar(50) OUTPUT
AS

DECLARE 	@vBOMID INT,
	@vBOMInternalRef VARCHAR(20),
	@iBOM_estimateParentID VARCHAR(20);

	SELECT @iBOM_estimateParentID=AE_AEID FROM BT_ESTIMATE_MASTER WHERE AE_internalRef=@iBOM_estimateParentRef;

	SET @vBOMID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/

INSERT INTO BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS
(	
	BOM_bomID,
	BOM_internalRef,
	BOM_estimateParentID, 
	BOM_investmentDriver, 
	BOM_name, 
	BOM_type, 

	BOM_priceBook, 
	BOM_taskType, 
	BOM_description, 

	BOM_openDate,  
	BOM_status, 
	BOM_generatedProject, 

	BOM_addToProject, 
	BOM_dummyProject, 
	BOM_customerContributionPercent, 

	BOM_createdBy, 
	BOM_dateCreated, 
	BOM_lastUpdatedBy, 
	BOM_lastUpdated, 
	BOM_deleted
)
VALUES
(
	@vBOMID,
	CONCAT('BOM', @vBOMID),
	@iBOM_estimateParentID, 
	@iBOM_investmentDriver, 
	@iBOM_name, 
	@iBOM_type, 

	@iBOM_priceBook, 
	@iBOM_taskType, 
	@iBOM_description, 

	getDate(), 
	@iBOM_status, 
	null, 

	@iBOM_addToProject, 
	@iBOM_dummyProject, 
	@iBOM_customerContributionPercent, 

	@userid, 
	getDate(), 
	@userID, 
	getDate(), 
	'N');

	INSERT INTO BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY
	 ([TC_TCID]
      ,[TC_parentBOMID]
      ,[TC_seq]
      ,[TC_priceBook]
      ,[TC_name]
      ,[TC_description]
      ,[TC_exclProj]
      ,[TC_totalCost]
      ,[TC_template]
      ,[TC_sourceTemplateTCID]
      ,[TC_dateCreated]
      ,[TC_createdBy]
      ,[TC_lastUpdated]
      ,[TC_lastUpdatedBy]
      ,[TC_deleted]
      ,[TC_totalCost_Oncost])

  SELECT  NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY OVER (ORDER BY TC_seq) /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/ as tcid,
	   @vBOMID
      ,[TC_seq]
      ,[TC_priceBook]
      ,[TC_name]
      ,[TC_description]
      ,[TC_exclProj]
      ,[TC_totalCost]
      ,[TC_template]
      ,[TC_TCID],        
		getDate(), 
		@userID, 
		getDate(),
		@userid,
       [TC_deleted]
      ,[TC_totalCost_Oncost]
  FROM MT_TASKCATEGORY where TC_priceBook=@iBOM_priceBook /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/;

  	

  INSERT INTO [BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS]([TCI_ID]
      ,[TCI_parentTCID]
      ,[TCI_priceBook]
      ,[TCI_type]
      ,[TCI_code]
      ,[TCI_sequence]
      ,[TCI_itemName]
      ,[TCI_materialsCost]
      ,[TCI_labourCost]
      ,[TCI_contractCost]
      ,[TCI_contestSplit]
      ,[TCI_nonContestSplit]
      ,[TCI_labourHours]
      ,[TCI_contractZone]
      ,[TCI_displayContest]
      ,[TCI_displayNonContest]
      ,[TCI_displayDesc]
      ,[TCI_contestValueMoney]
      ,[TCI_contestValueMoneyDefault]
      ,[TCI_contestValQty]
      ,[TCI_contestValQtyDefault]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValMoney]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValMoneyDefault]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValQty]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValQtyDefault]
      ,[TCI_description]
      ,[TCI_totalCost]
      ,[TCI_lovName]
      ,[TCI_itemToUpdate]
      ,[TCI_isTemplate]
      ,[TCI_selectedLOVValue]
      ,[TCI_sourceTemplateTCI_ID]
      ,[TCI_dateCreated]
      ,[TCI_createdBy]
      ,[TCI_lastUpdated]
      ,[TCI_lastUpdatedBy]
      ,[TCI_deleted]
      ,[TCI_calculatedTotalLabourHour]
      ,[TCI_labourApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_materialsApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_contractApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_totalCost_Oncost])

SELECT (SELECT MAX(TCI_ID) +1 FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS)+ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY TCI_ID)
	  ,(SELECT TC_TCID FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY WHERE TC_parentBOMID=@vBOMID and TC_sourceTemplateTCID=TCI_parentTCID)
      ,[TCI_priceBook]
      ,[TCI_type]
      ,[TCI_code]
      ,[TCI_sequence]
      ,[TCI_itemName]
      ,[TCI_materialsCost]
      ,[TCI_labourCost]
      ,[TCI_contractCost]
      ,[TCI_contestSplit]
      ,[TCI_nonContestSplit]
      ,[TCI_labourHours]
      ,[TCI_contractZone]
      ,[TCI_displayContest]
      ,[TCI_displayNonContest]
      ,[TCI_displayDesc]
      ,[TCI_contestValueMoney]
      ,[TCI_contestValueMoneyDefault]
      ,[TCI_contestValQty]
      ,[TCI_contestValQtyDefault]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValMoney]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValMoneyDefault]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValQty]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValQtyDefault]
      ,[TCI_description]
      ,[TCI_totalCost]
      ,[TCI_lovName]
      ,[TCI_itemToUpdate]
      ,[TCI_isTemplate]
      ,[TCI_selectedLOVValue]
      ,[TCI_sourceTemplateTCI_ID]
	 
	  ,getDate() 
	  ,@userID 
	  ,getDate() 
       ,@userid 
	  ,[TCI_deleted]
      ,[TCI_calculatedTotalLabourHour]
      ,[TCI_labourApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_materialsApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_contractApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_totalCost_Oncost]
      
  FROM [MT_PRICEBOOK]
  WHERE TCI_priceBook=@iBOM_priceBook order by  [TCI_sequence];




	SELECT CONCAT('BOM', @vBOMID);
GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

--EXEC [usp_AddBoMWithNewPriceBook] 'BOM357561','EST25000942',3780,'78','Bespoke <100m Projects - Yorkshire (Apr 2018)','115'
ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[usp_AddBoMWithNewPriceBook] 
	@iBOM_BOMReference varchar(50),
	@iBOM_estimateParentRef varchar(50),
	@userid int,
	@iNewPricebookValue VARCHAR(100),
	@iOldPricebookText VARCHAR(100),
	@iOldPricebookValue VARCHAR(50)
AS
-- =============================================
-- Author      : TCS
-- Created date: 05/01/2022
-- Created  By : Maynak Nandi
-- Description : Wave 4 COST UDPATE with diffent pricebook.
-- =============================================
DECLARE 	@vBOMID INT,
			@vBOMInternalRef VARCHAR(20),
			@iBOM_estimateParentID VARCHAR(20),
			@AE_assignedtodept int,
			@AE_designer int,
			@NewBoMID VARCHAR(50),
			@BOM_AEID INT;
			

			SET @BOM_AEID= CAST((SELECT SUBSTRING(@iBOM_BOMReference, 4, LEN(@iBOM_BOMReference)))AS INT);
	
			SELECT @iBOM_estimateParentID=AE_AEID FROM BT_ESTIMATE_MASTER WHERE AE_internalRef=@iBOM_estimateParentRef;
	
			SELECT @AE_designer=q.AE_designer, @AE_assignedtodept=q.AE_assignedToDept FROM BT_QUOTE_MASTER q,BT_ESTIMATE_MASTER e 
			WHERE q.AE_AEID=e.AE_ParentID and e.AE_AEID=@iBOM_estimateParentID
	
			SET @vBOMID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/

			SET @NewBoMID= CONCAT('BOM', @vBOMID)

			INSERT INTO BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS
			(	
				BOM_bomID,
				BOM_internalRef,
				BOM_estimateParentID, 
				BOM_investmentDriver,
				 
				BOM_name, 
				BOM_type, 
				BOM_priceBook, 
				BOM_taskType, 

				BOM_description, 
				BOM_openDate,  
				BOM_generatedProject, 
				BOM_addToProject, 

				BOM_dummyProject, 
				BOM_customerContributionPercent, 
				BOM_createdBy, 
				BOM_dateCreated, 

				BOM_lastUpdatedBy, 
				BOM_lastUpdated, 
				BOM_deleted,
				BOM_assignedToDesigner,

				BOM_assignedToDept,
				BOM_status,
				BOM_taskCategoryTotalCost,
				BOM_addUpliftPercent,

				BOM_deliveryProjectCost,
				BOM_addedUpliftMoney,
				BOM_customerContributionMoney,
				BOM_lockTaskCategories,

				BOM_addedUpliftDeliveryCost,
				BOM_applyMargin,

				BOM_customerContriReason,
				BOM_VatRate
			)

			SELECT
			@vBOMID,
			CONCAT('BOM', @vBOMID),
			@iBOM_estimateParentID,
			BOM_investmentDriver,

			BOM_name, 
			BOM_type,
			@iNewPricebookValue,
			BOM_taskType,

			BOM_description,
			getDate(),
			null,
			BOM_addToProject,

			BOM_dummyProject,
			BOM_customerContributionPercent,
			@userid,
			getdate(),

			@userID, 
			getDate(),
			'N',
			@AE_designer,

			@AE_assignedtodept,
			1,
			0,
			0,

			0,
			0,
			0,
			'N',

			0,
			'Y',

			BOM_customerContriReason,
			BOM_VatRate

			FROM BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS 
			WHERE BOM_internalRef=@iBOM_BOMReference

			INSERT INTO BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY
				 ([TC_TCID]
				  ,[TC_parentBOMID]
				  ,[TC_seq]
				  ,[TC_priceBook]
				  ,[TC_name]
				  ,[TC_description]
				  ,[TC_exclProj]
				  ,[TC_totalCost]
				  ,[TC_template]
				  ,[TC_sourceTemplateTCID]
				  ,[TC_dateCreated]
				  ,[TC_createdBy]
				  ,[TC_lastUpdated]
				  ,[TC_lastUpdatedBy]
				  ,[TC_deleted]
				  ,[TC_totalCost_Oncost])

			  SELECT  NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY OVER (ORDER BY TC_seq) /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/ as tcid,
				   @vBOMID
				  ,[TC_seq]
				  ,[TC_priceBook]
				  ,[TC_name]
				  ,[TC_description]
				  ,[TC_exclProj]
				  ,[TC_totalCost]
				  ,'N'
				  ,[TC_TCID],        
					getDate(), 
					@userID, 
					getDate(),
					@userid,
				   [TC_deleted]
				  ,[TC_totalCost_Oncost]
			  FROM MT_TASKCATEGORY where TC_priceBook=@iNewPricebookValue /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/;	


			INSERT INTO [BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS](
				 -- ,
				  [TCI_parentTCID]
				  ,[TCI_priceBook]
				  ,[TCI_type]
				  ,[TCI_code]
				  ,[TCI_sequence]
				  ,[TCI_itemName]
				  ,[TCI_materialsCost]
				  ,[TCI_labourCost]
				  ,[TCI_contractCost]
				  ,[TCI_contestSplit]
				  ,[TCI_nonContestSplit]
				  ,[TCI_labourHours]
				  ,[TCI_contractZone]
				  ,[TCI_displayContest]
				  ,[TCI_displayNonContest]
				  ,[TCI_displayDesc]
				  ,[TCI_contestValueMoney]
				  ,[TCI_contestValueMoneyDefault]
				  ,[TCI_contestValQty]
				  ,[TCI_contestValQtyDefault]
				  ,[TCI_nonContestValMoney]
				  ,[TCI_nonContestValMoneyDefault]
				  ,[TCI_nonContestValQty]
				  ,[TCI_nonContestValQtyDefault]
				  ,[TCI_description]
				  ,[TCI_totalCost]
				  ,[TCI_lovName]
				  ,[TCI_itemToUpdate]
				  ,[TCI_isTemplate]
				  ,[TCI_selectedLOVValue]
				  ,[TCI_sourceTemplateTCI_ID]
				  ,[TCI_dateCreated]
				  ,[TCI_createdBy]
				  ,[TCI_lastUpdated]
				  ,[TCI_lastUpdatedBy]
				  ,[TCI_deleted]
				  ,[TCI_calculatedTotalLabourHour]
				  ,[TCI_labourApplyOncost]
				  ,[TCI_materialsApplyOncost]
				  ,[TCI_contractApplyOncost]
				  ,[TCI_totalCost_Oncost])

	SELECT --(SELECT MAX(TCI_ID) +1 FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS)+ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY TCI_ID)
			--,
			(SELECT TC_TCID FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY M WHERE TC_parentBOMID=@vBOMID and M.TC_sourceTemplateTCID=tasks.TC_sourceTemplateTCID )
			,[TCI_priceBook]
			,[TCI_type]
			,[TCI_code]
			,asg.[TCAI_seq]
			,[TCI_itemName]
			,[TCI_materialsCost]
			,[TCI_labourCost]
			,[TCI_contractCost]
			,[TCI_contestSplit]
			,[TCI_nonContestSplit]
			,[TCI_labourHours]
			,[TCI_contractZone]
			,[TCI_displayContest]
			,[TCI_displayNonContest]
			,[TCI_displayDesc]
			,[TCI_contestValueMoney]
			,[TCI_contestValueMoneyDefault]
			,[TCI_contestValQty]
			,[TCI_contestValQtyDefault]
			,[TCI_nonContestValMoney]
			,[TCI_nonContestValMoneyDefault]
			,[TCI_nonContestValQty]
			,[TCI_nonContestValQtyDefault]
			,[TCI_description]
			,[TCI_totalCost]
			,[TCI_lovName]
			,[TCI_itemToUpdate]
			,'N'
			,[TCI_selectedLOVValue]
			,[TCI_sourceTemplateTCI_ID]
	 
			,getDate() 
			,@userID 
			,getDate() 
			,@userid 
			,[TCI_deleted]
			,[TCI_calculatedTotalLabourHour]
			,[TCI_labourApplyOncost]
			,[TCI_materialsApplyOncost]
			,[TCI_contractApplyOncost]
			,[TCI_totalCost_Oncost]
      
		FROM [MT_PRICEBOOK] PRICEBOOK,BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYASSIGNNEDITEMS asg,
		(SELECT * FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY tasks WHERE TC_parentBOMID=@vBOMID  and tasks.TC_priceBook=@iNewPricebookValue
		) TASKS
		WHERE PRICEBOOK.TCI_ID=asg.TCAI_ITEMID  
		AND asg.TCAI_TASKCATEGORYID = tasks.TC_sourceTemplateTCID 
		AND asg.TCAI_deleted = 'N'   
		order by  [TCI_sequence];


			SELECT CONCAT('BOM', @vBOMID);

			CREATE TABLE #temp_TC_OldPriceBook (
					ID INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
					TC_TCID INT,
					TC_priceBook VARCHAR(50),
			);

			CREATE TABLE #temp_TCI_OldPriceBook (
				ID INT IDENTITY(1,1) PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
				TCI_ID INT,
				TCI_parentTCID INT,
				TCI_priceBook VARCHAR(50),
				TCI_code VARCHAR(50),
				TCI_contestValQty INT,
				TCI_noncontestValQty INT,
				TCI_contestValueMoney MONEY,
				TCI_nonContestValMoney MONEY,
				TCI_itemName VARCHAR(100)
			);

			--Fetching Old BoM Taskcategory Records.
			INSERT INTO #temp_TC_OldPriceBook(TC_TCID,TC_priceBook)
			SELECT TC_TCID,TC_priceBook FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY WHERE TC_parentBOMID=@BOM_AEID--@vBOMID;

			SELECT 1 AS '1'
			SELECT * FROM #temp_TC_OldPriceBook
		
			DECLARE @TCMinCount INT=1,
					@TCMaxCount INT,
					@vTC_TCID INT;

			DECLARE @TCIMinCount INT=1,
							@TCIMaxCount INT,
							@vTCI_contestValQty INT,
							@vTCI_noncontestValQty INT,
							@vTCI_contestValueMoney MONEY,
							@vTCI_nonContestValMoney MONEY,
							@vTCI_itemName VARCHAR(100),
							@vTCI_code VARCHAR(50)
				
				   DECLARE @vOldTCI_contestValQty INT,
						   @vOldTCI_noncontestValQty INT,
						   @vOldTCI_contestValueMoney MONEY,
						   @vOldTCI_nonContestValMoney MONEY,
						   @vOldTCI_itemName VARCHAR(100),
						   @vOldTCI_code VARCHAR(50)

			SELECT @TCMaxCount= COUNT(1) FROM #temp_TC_OldPriceBook
		
			WHILE ( @TCMinCount <= @TCMaxCount)
			BEGIN
					SELECT @vTC_TCID= TC_TCID FROM #temp_TC_OldPriceBook WHERE ID=@TCMinCount

					INSERT INTO #temp_TCI_OldPriceBook(TCI_ID,TCI_parentTCID,TCI_priceBook,TCI_code,TCI_itemName,TCI_contestValQty,TCI_noncontestValQty,TCI_contestValueMoney,TCI_nonContestValMoney)
					SELECT TCI_ID,TCI_parentTCID,TCI_priceBook,TCI_code,TCI_itemName,TCI_contestValQty,TCI_noncontestValQty,TCI_contestValueMoney,TCI_nonContestValMoney 
					FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS 
					WHERE TCI_parentTCID=@vTC_TCID AND (coalesce(TCI_contestValQty,0)+ coalesce(TCI_noncontestValQty,0) + coalesce(TCI_contestValueMoney,0)+ 
						coalesce(TCI_nonContestValMoney,0))>0;

					SELECT 2 AS '2'
					SELECT * FROM #temp_TCI_OldPriceBook

							SET @TCIMinCount =1;
							SET @TCIMaxCount=0;
							SET @vTCI_contestValQty=0;
							SET @vTCI_noncontestValQty=0;
							SET @vTCI_contestValueMoney=0;
							SET @vTCI_nonContestValMoney=0;
							SET @vTCI_itemName='';
							SET @vTCI_code=''
				
						   SET @vOldTCI_contestValQty =0;
						   SET @vOldTCI_noncontestValQty =0;
						   SET @vOldTCI_contestValueMoney =0;
						   SET @vOldTCI_nonContestValMoney =0;
						   SET @vOldTCI_itemName='';
						   SET @vOldTCI_code='';
				
					SELECT @TCIMaxCount= COUNT(1) FROM #temp_TCI_OldPriceBook
					IF(@TCIMaxCount>0)
						BEGIN
							WHILE ( @TCIMinCount <= @TCIMaxCount)
							BEGIN
								SELECT @TCIMinCount AS 'INNER LOOP'
								--Old BoM
								SELECT 
								@vOldTCI_contestValQty = isnull(TCI_contestValQty,0),
								@vOldTCI_noncontestValQty=isnull(TCI_noncontestValQty,0),
								@vOldTCI_contestValueMoney=isnull(TCI_contestValueMoney,0),
								@vOldTCI_nonContestValMoney=isnull(TCI_nonContestValMoney,0),
								@vOldTCI_itemName=isnull(TCI_itemName,'-'),
								@vOldTCI_code=isnull(TCI_code,'-') 
								FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS WHERE TCI_ID IN (SELECT LTRIM(RTRIM(TCI_ID)) FROM #temp_TCI_OldPriceBook WHERE ID=@TCIMinCount/*@vBOMID*/) 
								AND LTRIM(RTRIM(TCI_itemName))=(SELECT LTRIM(RTRIM(TCI_itemName)) FROM #temp_TCI_OldPriceBook WHERE ID=@TCIMinCount) 
								AND LTRIM(RTRIM(TCI_code))=(SELECT LTRIM(RTRIM(TCI_code)) FROM #temp_TCI_OldPriceBook WHERE ID=@TCIMinCount)
					
								SELECT 3 AS '3'
								SELECT 
								isnull(TCI_contestValQty,0),
								isnull(TCI_noncontestValQty,0),
								isnull(TCI_contestValueMoney,0),
								isnull(TCI_nonContestValMoney,0),
								isnull(TCI_itemName,'-'),
								isnull(TCI_code,'-')   
								FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS WHERE TCI_ID IN (SELECT LTRIM(RTRIM(TCI_ID)) FROM #temp_TCI_OldPriceBook WHERE ID=@TCIMinCount/*@vBOMID*/) 
								AND LTRIM(RTRIM(TCI_itemName))=(SELECT LTRIM(RTRIM(TCI_itemName)) FROM #temp_TCI_OldPriceBook WHERE ID=@TCIMinCount) 
								AND LTRIM(RTRIM(TCI_code))=(SELECT LTRIM(RTRIM(TCI_code)) FROM #temp_TCI_OldPriceBook WHERE ID=@TCIMinCount)
								
								--New BoM
								 SELECT @vTCI_contestValQty = isnull(TCI_contestValQty,0),
										@vTCI_noncontestValQty=isnull(TCI_noncontestValQty,0),
										@vTCI_contestValueMoney=isnull(TCI_contestValueMoney,0),
										@vTCI_nonContestValMoney=isnull(TCI_nonContestValMoney,0),
										@vTCI_itemName=isnull(@vTCI_itemName,'-'),
										@vTCI_code=isnull(TCI_code,'-')
										FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS WHERE TCI_parentTCID IN (SELECT  TC_TCID FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY 
										WHERE TC_parentBOMID=@vBOMID/*@BOM_AEID*/) AND TCI_itemName=LTRIM(RTRIM(@vOldTCI_itemName)) 
										AND TCI_code=LTRIM(RTRIM(@vOldTCI_code))
					
								SELECT 4 AS '4'
								SELECT @vOldTCI_itemName AS OldTCI_itemName
								SELECT @vOldTCI_code AS OldTCI_code
								SELECT 
								isnull(TCI_contestValQty,0),
								isnull(TCI_noncontestValQty,0),
								isnull(TCI_contestValueMoney,0),
								isnull(TCI_nonContestValMoney,0),
								isnull(TCI_itemName,'-'),
								isnull(TCI_code,'-') 
								FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS WHERE TCI_parentTCID IN (SELECT  TC_TCID FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY 
										WHERE TC_parentBOMID=@vBOMID/*@BOM_AEID*/) AND ltrim(rtrim(TCI_itemName))=LTRIM(RTRIM(@vOldTCI_itemName)) 
										AND ltrim(rtrim(TCI_code))=LTRIM(RTRIM(@vOldTCI_code))

								--IF( (coalesce(@vOldTCI_contestValQty,0)+ coalesce(@vOldTCI_noncontestValQty,0) + coalesce(@vOldTCI_contestValueMoney,0)+ 
								--coalesce(@vOldTCI_nonContestValMoney,0) )>0)
									--BEGIN
										SELECT 5 AS '5'
										SELECT  TCI_contestValQty,TCI_noncontestValQty,TCI_contestValueMoney,TCI_nonContestValMoney
										FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS 
										WHERE TCI_parentTCID IN (SELECT TC_TCID FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY 
										WHERE TC_parentBOMID=@vBOMID --New BoM Update
										AND TCI_itemName=LTRIM(RTRIM(@vOldTCI_itemName)) AND TCI_code=LTRIM(RTRIM(@vOldTCI_code)))
							
										UPDATE BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS SET TCI_contestValQty= @vOldTCI_contestValQty,
										TCI_noncontestValQty=@vOldTCI_noncontestValQty,
										TCI_contestValueMoney=@vOldTCI_contestValueMoney,
										TCI_nonContestValMoney=@vOldTCI_nonContestValMoney
										--WHERE TCI_ID=(SELECT TCI_ID FROM #temp_TCI_OldPriceBook WHERE ID=@TCIMinCount)
										WHERE TCI_parentTCID IN (SELECT TC_TCID FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY 
										WHERE TC_parentBOMID=@vBOMID --New BoM Update
										AND TCI_itemName=LTRIM(RTRIM(@vOldTCI_itemName)) AND TCI_code=LTRIM(RTRIM(@vOldTCI_code)))
									--END
								--ELSE
								--	BEGIN
								--		UPDATE BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS SET 
								--		TCI_contestValQty= 0,
								--		TCI_noncontestValQty=0,
								--		TCI_contestValueMoney=0,
								--		TCI_nonContestValMoney=0
								--		--WHERE TCI_ID=(SELECT TCI_ID FROM #temp_TCI_OldPriceBook WHERE ID=@TCIMinCount)
								--		WHERE TCI_parentTCID IN (SELECT TC_TCID FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY WHERE TC_parentBOMID=@vBOMID --New BoM Update
								--		AND TCI_itemName=LTRIM(RTRIM(@vOldTCI_itemName)) AND TCI_code=LTRIM(RTRIM(@vOldTCI_code)))
								--	END
								SET @TCIMinCount  = @TCIMinCount  + 1
								
							END
							TRUNCATE TABLE #temp_TCI_OldPriceBook
						END
				PRINT 'OUTER LOOP'
				SET @TCMinCount  = @TCMinCount  + 1
			END

		DROP TABLE #temp_TC_OldPriceBook
		DROP TABLE #temp_TCI_OldPriceBook




GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

-- ---- SP_UPDATE_BOMPRICEBOOK (2 sites)
IF @@TRANCOUNT = 0 SET NOEXEC ON;
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_UPDATE_BOMPRICEBOOK] 
	@iBOM_Ref varchar(50),
	@iBOM_estimateParentRef varchar(20),
	@iBOM_investmentDriver varchar(50), 
	@iBOM_name varchar(100), 
	@iBOM_type varchar(50), 
	@iAgeOfAsset int = Null ,
	@iBOM_priceBook varchar(50), 
	@iBOM_taskType varchar(50), 
	@iBOM_description varchar(2000),
	@iBOM_status varchar(50), 
	@iBOM_addToProject int, 
	@iBOM_dummyProject varchar(1), 
	@iBOM_customerContributionPercent float,
	@iBOM_uplift  float=0, --changes for uplift 
	@userid int,
	@iBOM_CustContriReason varchar(2000) = NULL,
	@vatRate int,
	@newBOMID varchar(50) OUTPUT
	 --,@DateCompleted smallDateTime=NULL
AS

DECLARE 	@vBOMID INT,
	@vBOMInternalRef VARCHAR(20),
	@iBOM_estimateParentID VARCHAR(20),
	@AE_assignedtodept int,
	@AE_designer int;
	
	SELECT @iBOM_estimateParentID=AE_AEID FROM BT_ESTIMATE_MASTER WHERE AE_internalRef=@iBOM_estimateParentRef;
	--SELECT @AE_designer=AE_designer, @AE_assignedtodept=AE_assignedToDept from BT_QUOTE_MASTER WHERE AE_AEID=@iBOM_estimateParentID
SELECT @AE_designer=q.AE_designer, @AE_assignedtodept=q.AE_assignedToDept from BT_QUOTE_MASTER q,BT_ESTIMATE_MASTER e WHERE q.AE_AEID=e.AE_ParentID and e.AE_AEID=@iBOM_estimateParentID
	SELECT @vBOMID=COALESCE(BOM_bomID,-1) 
	FROM BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS where BOM_internalRef=@iBOM_Ref;

DELETE FROM BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS WHERE BOM_bomID=COALESCE(@vBOMID,-1)

INSERT INTO BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS
(	
	BOM_bomID,
	BOM_internalRef,
	BOM_estimateParentID, 
	BOM_investmentDriver, 
	BOM_name,

	BOM_createdBy,

	BOM_deliveryProjectCost,

	BOM_customerContriReason,

	--,BOM_completeDate
	--,BOM_Uplift--changes for uplift
)
VALUES

	@iBOM_estimateParentID, 
	@iBOM_investmentDriver, 
	@iBOM_name, 
	@iBOM_type, 

	@iBOM_priceBook, 
	@iBOM_taskType, 
	@iBOM_description, 

	getDate(), 
	null, 

	@iBOM_addToProject, 
	@iBOM_dummyProject, 
	@iBOM_customerContributionPercent, 

	@userid, 
	getDate(), 
	@userID, 
	getDate(), 
	'N',
	
	@AE_designer,
	@AE_assignedtodept,
	1,
	0,0,0,0,0,'N',0,'Y',
	@iBOM_CustContriReason,
	@iAgeOfAsset,
	@vatRate
	--,@DateCompleted
	--,@iBOM_uplift--changes for uplift
	);

DELETE FROM [BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS] WHERE [TCI_parentTCID] IN (
SELECT TC_TCID FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY WHERE [TC_parentBOMID]=COALESCE(@vBOMID,-1))

DELETE FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY WHERE [TC_parentBOMID]=COALESCE(@vBOMID,-1)

	INSERT INTO BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY
	 ([TC_TCID]
      ,[TC_parentBOMID]
      ,[TC_seq]
      ,[TC_priceBook]
      ,[TC_name]
      ,[TC_description]
      ,[TC_exclProj]
      ,[TC_totalCost]
      ,[TC_template]
      ,[TC_sourceTemplateTCID]
      ,[TC_dateCreated]
      ,[TC_createdBy]
      ,[TC_lastUpdated]
      ,[TC_lastUpdatedBy]
      ,[TC_deleted]
      ,[TC_totalCost_Oncost])

  SELECT  NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY OVER (ORDER BY TC_seq) /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/ as tcid,
	   @vBOMID
      ,[TC_seq]
      ,[TC_priceBook]
      ,[TC_name]
      ,[TC_description]
      ,[TC_exclProj]
      ,[TC_totalCost]
      ,'N'
      ,[TC_TCID],        
		getDate(), 
		@userID, 
		getDate(),
		@userid,
       [TC_deleted]
      ,[TC_totalCost_Oncost]
  FROM MT_TASKCATEGORY where TC_priceBook=@iBOM_priceBook /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/;	


  INSERT INTO [BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS](--[TCI_ID]
     -- ,
	  [TCI_parentTCID]
      ,[TCI_priceBook]
      ,[TCI_type]
      ,[TCI_code]
      ,[TCI_sequence]
      ,[TCI_itemName]
      ,[TCI_materialsCost]
      ,[TCI_labourCost]
      ,[TCI_contractCost]
      ,[TCI_contestSplit]
      ,[TCI_nonContestSplit]
      ,[TCI_labourHours]
      ,[TCI_contractZone]
      ,[TCI_displayContest]
      ,[TCI_displayNonContest]
      ,[TCI_displayDesc]
      ,[TCI_contestValueMoney]
      ,[TCI_contestValueMoneyDefault]
      ,[TCI_contestValQty]
      ,[TCI_contestValQtyDefault]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValMoney]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValMoneyDefault]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValQty]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValQtyDefault]
      ,[TCI_description]
      ,[TCI_totalCost]
      ,[TCI_lovName]
      ,[TCI_itemToUpdate]
      ,[TCI_isTemplate]
      ,[TCI_selectedLOVValue]
      ,[TCI_sourceTemplateTCI_ID]
      ,[TCI_dateCreated]
      ,[TCI_createdBy]
      ,[TCI_lastUpdated]
      ,[TCI_lastUpdatedBy]
      ,[TCI_deleted]
      ,[TCI_calculatedTotalLabourHour]
      ,[TCI_labourApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_materialsApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_contractApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_totalCost_Oncost])

SELECT --(SELECT MAX(TCI_ID) +1 FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS)+ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY TCI_ID)
	  --,
	  (SELECT TC_TCID FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY M WHERE TC_parentBOMID=@vBOMID and M.TC_sourceTemplateTCID=tasks.TC_sourceTemplateTCID )
      ,[TCI_priceBook]
      ,[TCI_type]
      ,[TCI_code]
      , asg.[TCAI_seq]
      ,[TCI_itemName]
      ,[TCI_materialsCost]
      ,[TCI_labourCost]
      ,[TCI_contractCost]
      ,[TCI_contestSplit]
      ,[TCI_nonContestSplit]
      ,[TCI_labourHours]
      ,[TCI_contractZone]
      ,[TCI_displayContest]
      ,[TCI_displayNonContest]
      ,[TCI_displayDesc]
      ,[TCI_contestValueMoney]
      ,[TCI_contestValueMoneyDefault]
      ,[TCI_contestValQty]
      ,[TCI_contestValQtyDefault]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValMoney]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValMoneyDefault]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValQty]
      ,[TCI_nonContestValQtyDefault]
      ,[TCI_description]
      ,[TCI_totalCost]
      ,[TCI_lovName]
      ,[TCI_itemToUpdate]
      ,'N'
      ,[TCI_selectedLOVValue]
      ,[TCI_sourceTemplateTCI_ID]
	 
	  ,getDate() 
	 ,@userID 
	  ,getDate() 
      ,@userid 
	  ,[TCI_deleted]
      ,[TCI_calculatedTotalLabourHour]
      ,[TCI_labourApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_materialsApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_contractApplyOncost]
      ,[TCI_totalCost_Oncost]
      
  FROM [MT_PRICEBOOK] PRICEBOOK,BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYASSIGNNEDITEMS asg,
   (SELECT * FROM BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY tasks WHERE TC_parentBOMID=@vBOMID  and tasks.TC_priceBook=@iBOM_priceBook
 ) TASKS
  WHERE PRICEBOOK.TCI_ID=asg.TCAI_ITEMID  
  AND asg.TCAI_TASKCATEGORYID = tasks.TC_sourceTemplateTCID 
 AND asg.TCAI_deleted = 'N'   
 order by  [TCI_sequence];


	SELECT CONCAT('BOM', @vBOMID);
GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;

-- [SP_INSERT_GETBOM] 'BOM327294','EST25000153',3140
ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_INSERT_GETBOM] 
@bom_ref varchar(20),
@est_ref varchar(20),
@UserId int
AS 
BEGIN

	DECLARE @vBOMID INT,
			@iBOM_estimateParentID VARCHAR(20),
			@AE_assignedtodept int,
			@AE_designer int;
	
	SELECT @iBOM_estimateParentID=AE_AEID FROM BT_ESTIMATE_MASTER WHERE AE_internalRef=@est_ref;
	SELECT @AE_designer=q.AE_designer, @AE_assignedtodept=q.AE_assignedToDept from BT_QUOTE_MASTER q,BT_ESTIMATE_MASTER e WHERE q.AE_AEID=e.AE_ParentID and e.AE_AEID=@iBOM_estimateParentID
	SET @vBOMID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/

	INSERT INTO BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS
	(	BOM_bomID,	BOM_internalRef,	BOM_estimateParentID,	BOM_investmentDriver, 
		BOM_name, 	BOM_type,	BOM_priceBook, 	BOM_taskType, 	BOM_description, 
		BOM_openDate,	BOM_generatedProject, 	BOM_addToProject, 	BOM_dummyProject, 
		BOM_customerContributionPercent, 	BOM_createdBy, 	BOM_dateCreated,	BOM_lastUpdatedBy, 
		BOM_lastUpdated, 	BOM_deleted,	BOM_assignedToDesigner,	BOM_assignedToDept,	BOM_status,	
		BOM_taskCategoryTotalCost,	BOM_addUpliftPercent,	BOM_deliveryProjectCost,
		BOM_addedUpliftMoney,BOM_customerContributionMoney,BOM_lockTaskCategories,BOM_addedUpliftDeliveryCost,BOM_applyMargin
	) 
	SELECT @vBOMID,CONCAT('BOM',@vBOMID),@iBOM_estimateParentID,BOM_investmentDriver, 
		BOM_name, 	BOM_type,	BOM_priceBook, 	BOM_taskType, 	BOM_description, 
		BOM_openDate,	NULL, 	0, 	'N', 
		BOM_customerContributionPercent, 	@UserId, 	getdate(),	@UserId, 
		getdate(), 	BOM_deleted,	@AE_designer,	@AE_assignedtodept,	BOM_status,	
		BOM_taskCategoryTotalCost,	BOM_addUpliftPercent,	BOM_deliveryProjectCost,
		BOM_addedUpliftMoney,BOM_customerContributionMoney,BOM_lockTaskCategories,BOM_addedUpliftDeliveryCost,BOM_applyMargin
		FROM BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS where BOM_internalRef=@bom_ref;
	
--select * from BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY where TC_parentBOMID=(select bom_bomid from BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS where bom_internalref=@bom_ref)

	DECLARE @TC_TCID INT,@pkeyTC_TCID INT,@TCI_ID INT,@pkeyTCI_ID INT;
	DECLARE c_TaskCategory CURSOR
    FOR  select TC_TCID from BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY where [TC_deleted] ='N' 
	and TC_parentBOMID=(select bom_bomid from BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS where bom_internalref=@bom_ref); 
   
	OPEN c_TaskCategory; 
   
	FETCH NEXT FROM c_TaskCategory INTO @TC_TCID;

	WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
		SET @pkeyTC_TCID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/
    
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY]
			   ([TC_TCID]           ,[TC_parentBOMID]           ,[TC_seq]           ,[TC_priceBook]
			   ,[TC_name]           ,[TC_description]           ,[TC_exclProj]           ,[TC_totalCost]
			   ,[TC_template]           ,[TC_sourceTemplateTCID]           ,[TC_dateCreated]           ,[TC_createdBy]
			   ,[TC_lastUpdated]           ,[TC_lastUpdatedBy]           ,[TC_deleted]           ,[TC_totalCost_Oncost])

			   SELECT  @pkeyTC_TCID           ,@vBOMID           ,[TC_seq]           ,[TC_priceBook]
			   ,[TC_name]           ,[TC_description]           ,[TC_exclProj]           ,[TC_totalCost]
			   ,[TC_template]           ,[TC_sourceTemplateTCID]           ,getdate()           ,@UserId
			   ,getdate()           ,@UserId           ,[TC_deleted]           ,[TC_totalCost_Oncost] from [BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY] 
		WHERE TC_TCID = @TC_TCID ;

			DECLARE c_TaskCategoryItem CURSOR
			FOR  select TCI_ID from BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS where [TCI_deleted] ='N' and TCI_parentTCID=@TC_TCID; 
   
			OPEN c_TaskCategoryItem;
			FETCH NEXT FROM c_TaskCategoryItem INTO @TCI_ID;

				WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
				BEGIN
				select @pkeyTCI_ID=max(TCI_ID)+1 from BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS;
    
				INSERT INTO [dbo].BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS
					   ([TCI_parentTCID]           ,[TCI_priceBook]           ,[TCI_type]           ,[TCI_code]
					   ,[TCI_sequence]           ,[TCI_itemName]           ,[TCI_materialsCost]           ,[TCI_labourCost]
					   ,[TCI_contractCost]           ,[TCI_contestSplit]           ,[TCI_nonContestSplit]           ,[TCI_labourHours]
					   ,[TCI_contractZone]           ,[TCI_displayContest]           ,[TCI_displayNonContest]           ,[TCI_displayDesc]
					   ,[TCI_contestValueMoney]           ,[TCI_contestValueMoneyDefault]           ,[TCI_contestValQty]           ,[TCI_contestValQtyDefault]
					   ,[TCI_nonContestValMoney]           ,[TCI_nonContestValMoneyDefault]           ,[TCI_nonContestValQty]           ,[TCI_nonContestValQtyDefault]
					   ,[TCI_description]           ,[TCI_totalCost]           ,[TCI_lovName]           ,[TCI_itemToUpdate]
					   ,[TCI_isTemplate]           ,[TCI_selectedLOVValue]           ,[TCI_sourceTemplateTCI_ID]           ,[TCI_dateCreated]
					   ,[TCI_createdBy]           ,[TCI_lastUpdated]           ,[TCI_lastUpdatedBy]           ,[TCI_deleted]
					   ,[TCI_calculatedTotalLabourHour]           ,[TCI_labourApplyOncost]           ,[TCI_materialsApplyOncost]           ,[TCI_contractApplyOncost]
					   ,[TCI_totalCost_Oncost])

					   SELECT  @pkeyTC_TCID           ,[TCI_priceBook]           ,[TCI_type]           ,[TCI_code]
					   ,[TCI_sequence]           ,[TCI_itemName]           ,[TCI_materialsCost]           ,[TCI_labourCost]
					   ,[TCI_contractCost]           ,[TCI_contestSplit]           ,[TCI_nonContestSplit]           ,[TCI_labourHours]
					   ,[TCI_contractZone]           ,[TCI_displayContest]           ,[TCI_displayNonContest]           ,[TCI_displayDesc]
					   ,[TCI_contestValueMoney]           ,[TCI_contestValueMoneyDefault]           ,[TCI_contestValQty]           ,[TCI_contestValQtyDefault]
					   ,[TCI_nonContestValMoney]           ,[TCI_nonContestValMoneyDefault]           ,[TCI_nonContestValQty]           ,[TCI_nonContestValQtyDefault]
					   ,[TCI_description]           ,[TCI_totalCost]           ,[TCI_lovName]           ,[TCI_itemToUpdate]
					   ,[TCI_isTemplate]           ,[TCI_selectedLOVValue]           ,[TCI_sourceTemplateTCI_ID]           ,getdate()
					   ,@UserId           ,getdate()           ,@UserId           ,[TCI_deleted]
					   ,[TCI_calculatedTotalLabourHour]           ,[TCI_labourApplyOncost]           ,[TCI_materialsApplyOncost]           ,[TCI_contractApplyOncost]
					   ,[TCI_totalCost_Oncost] from [BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS] 
				WHERE TCI_ID = @TCI_ID ;

				FETCH NEXT FROM c_TaskCategoryItem INTO @TCI_ID;
				END;

			CLOSE c_TaskCategoryItem;
			DEALLOCATE c_TaskCategoryItem;
	 FETCH NEXT FROM c_TaskCategory INTO @TC_TCID;
	 END;
	 CLOSE c_TaskCategory
	 DEALLOCATE c_TaskCategory;


EXEC SP_UPDATE_QUOTE_FROM_BOM @est_ref, @userid;

END
GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

-- ---- USP_INSERT_GETBOM_FQUO (2 sites)
IF @@TRANCOUNT = 0 SET NOEXEC ON;
GO
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO


-- [SP_INSERT_GETBOM] 'BOM327294','EST25000153',3140
ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[USP_INSERT_GETBOM_FQUO] 
@bom_ref varchar(20),
@est_ref varchar(20),
@UserId int,
@vPriceBook varchar(20)
AS
-- =============================================
-- Author      : TCS
-- Created date: 05/01/2022
-- Created  By : Maynak Nandi
-- Description : Wave 4 ADD BoM from GET BoM functionility.
-- ============================================= 
BEGIN

	DECLARE @vBOMID INT,
			@iBOM_estimateParentID VARCHAR(20),
			@AE_assignedtodept int,
			@AE_designer int;
	
	SELECT @iBOM_estimateParentID=AE_AEID FROM BT_ESTIMATE_MASTER WHERE AE_internalRef=@est_ref;
	SELECT @AE_designer=q.AE_designer, @AE_assignedtodept=q.AE_assignedToDept from BT_QUOTE_MASTER q,BT_ESTIMATE_MASTER e WHERE q.AE_AEID=e.AE_ParentID and e.AE_AEID=@iBOM_estimateParentID
	SET @vBOMID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/

	INSERT INTO BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS
	(	BOM_bomID,	BOM_internalRef,	BOM_estimateParentID,	BOM_investmentDriver, 
		BOM_name, 	BOM_type,	BOM_priceBook, 	BOM_taskType, 	BOM_description, 
		BOM_openDate,	BOM_generatedProject, 	BOM_addToProject, 	BOM_dummyProject, 
		BOM_customerContributionPercent, 	BOM_createdBy, 	BOM_dateCreated,	BOM_lastUpdatedBy, 
		BOM_lastUpdated, 	BOM_deleted,	BOM_assignedToDesigner,	BOM_assignedToDept,	BOM_status,	
		BOM_taskCategoryTotalCost,	BOM_addUpliftPercent,	BOM_deliveryProjectCost,
		BOM_addedUpliftMoney,BOM_customerContributionMoney,BOM_lockTaskCategories,BOM_addedUpliftDeliveryCost,BOM_applyMargin,BOM_customerContriReason
		,BOM_VatRate
	) 
	SELECT @vBOMID,CONCAT('BOM',@vBOMID),@iBOM_estimateParentID,BOM_investmentDriver, 
		BOM_name, 	BOM_type,	@vPriceBook, 	BOM_taskType, 	BOM_description, 
		BOM_openDate,	NULL, 	0, 	'N', 
		BOM_customerContributionPercent, 	@UserId, 	getdate(),	@UserId, 
		getdate(), 	BOM_deleted,	@AE_designer,	@AE_assignedtodept,	BOM_status,	
		BOM_taskCategoryTotalCost,	BOM_addUpliftPercent,	BOM_deliveryProjectCost,
		BOM_addedUpliftMoney,BOM_customerContributionMoney,BOM_lockTaskCategories,BOM_addedUpliftDeliveryCost,BOM_applyMargin,BOM_customerContriReason
		,BOM_VatRate
		FROM BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS where BOM_internalRef=@bom_ref;
	
--select * from BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY where TC_parentBOMID=(select bom_bomid from BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS where bom_internalref=@bom_ref)

	DECLARE @TC_TCID INT,@pkeyTC_TCID INT,@TCI_ID INT,@pkeyTCI_ID INT;
	DECLARE c_TaskCategory CURSOR
    FOR  select TC_TCID from BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY where [TC_deleted] ='N' 
	and TC_parentBOMID=(select bom_bomid from BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS where bom_internalref=@bom_ref); 
   
	OPEN c_TaskCategory; 
   
	FETCH NEXT FROM c_TaskCategory INTO @TC_TCID;

	WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
    BEGIN
		SET @pkeyTC_TCID = NEXT VALUE FOR dbo.SEQ_BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY; /*QPID_OPT V4-10*/
    
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY]
			   ([TC_TCID]           ,[TC_parentBOMID]           ,[TC_seq]           ,[TC_priceBook]
			   ,[TC_name]           ,[TC_description]           ,[TC_exclProj]           ,[TC_totalCost]
			   ,[TC_template]           ,[TC_sourceTemplateTCID]           ,[TC_dateCreated]           ,[TC_createdBy]
			   ,[TC_lastUpdated]           ,[TC_lastUpdatedBy]           ,[TC_deleted]           ,[TC_totalCost_Oncost])

			   SELECT  @pkeyTC_TCID           ,@vBOMID           ,[TC_seq]           ,@vPriceBook
			   ,[TC_name]           ,[TC_description]           ,[TC_exclProj]           ,[TC_totalCost]
			   ,[TC_template]           ,[TC_sourceTemplateTCID]           ,getdate()           ,@UserId
			   ,getdate()           ,@UserId           ,[TC_deleted]           ,[TC_totalCost_Oncost] from [BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY] 
		WHERE TC_TCID = @TC_TCID ;

			DECLARE c_TaskCategoryItem CURSOR
			FOR  select TCI_ID from BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS where [TCI_deleted] ='N' and TCI_parentTCID=@TC_TCID; 
   
			OPEN c_TaskCategoryItem;
			FETCH NEXT FROM c_TaskCategoryItem INTO @TCI_ID;

				WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS = 0
				BEGIN
				select @pkeyTCI_ID=max(TCI_ID)+1 from BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS;
    
				INSERT INTO [dbo].BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS
					   ([TCI_parentTCID]           ,[TCI_priceBook]           ,[TCI_type]           ,[TCI_code]
					   ,[TCI_sequence]           ,[TCI_itemName]           ,[TCI_materialsCost]           ,[TCI_labourCost]
					   ,[TCI_contractCost]           ,[TCI_contestSplit]           ,[TCI_nonContestSplit]           ,[TCI_labourHours]
					   ,[TCI_contractZone]           ,[TCI_displayContest]           ,[TCI_displayNonContest]           ,[TCI_displayDesc]
					   ,[TCI_contestValueMoney]           ,[TCI_contestValueMoneyDefault]           ,[TCI_contestValQty]           ,[TCI_contestValQtyDefault]
					   ,[TCI_nonContestValMoney]           ,[TCI_nonContestValMoneyDefault]           ,[TCI_nonContestValQty]           ,[TCI_nonContestValQtyDefault]
					   ,[TCI_description]           ,[TCI_totalCost]           ,[TCI_lovName]           ,[TCI_itemToUpdate]
					   ,[TCI_isTemplate]           ,[TCI_selectedLOVValue]           ,[TCI_sourceTemplateTCI_ID]           ,[TCI_dateCreated]
					   ,[TCI_createdBy]           ,[TCI_lastUpdated]           ,[TCI_lastUpdatedBy]           ,[TCI_deleted]
					   ,[TCI_calculatedTotalLabourHour]           ,[TCI_labourApplyOncost]           ,[TCI_materialsApplyOncost]           ,[TCI_contractApplyOncost]
					   ,[TCI_totalCost_Oncost])

					   SELECT  @pkeyTC_TCID           ,@vPriceBook           ,[TCI_type]           ,[TCI_code]
					   ,[TCI_sequence]           ,[TCI_itemName]           ,[TCI_materialsCost]           ,[TCI_labourCost]
					   ,[TCI_contractCost]           ,[TCI_contestSplit]           ,[TCI_nonContestSplit]           ,[TCI_labourHours]
					   ,[TCI_contractZone]           ,[TCI_displayContest]           ,[TCI_displayNonContest]           ,[TCI_displayDesc]
					   ,[TCI_contestValueMoney]           ,[TCI_contestValueMoneyDefault]           ,[TCI_contestValQty]           ,[TCI_contestValQtyDefault]
					   ,[TCI_nonContestValMoney]           ,[TCI_nonContestValMoneyDefault]           ,[TCI_nonContestValQty]           ,[TCI_nonContestValQtyDefault]
					   ,[TCI_description]           ,[TCI_totalCost]           ,[TCI_lovName]           ,[TCI_itemToUpdate]
					   ,[TCI_isTemplate]           ,[TCI_selectedLOVValue]           ,[TCI_sourceTemplateTCI_ID]           ,getdate()
					   ,@UserId           ,getdate()           ,@UserId           ,[TCI_deleted]
					   ,[TCI_calculatedTotalLabourHour]           ,[TCI_labourApplyOncost]           ,[TCI_materialsApplyOncost]           ,[TCI_contractApplyOncost]
					   ,[TCI_totalCost_Oncost] from [BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORYITEMS] 
				WHERE TCI_ID = @TCI_ID ;

				FETCH NEXT FROM c_TaskCategoryItem INTO @TCI_ID;
				END;

			CLOSE c_TaskCategoryItem;
			DEALLOCATE c_TaskCategoryItem;
	 FETCH NEXT FROM c_TaskCategory INTO @TC_TCID;
	 END;
	 CLOSE c_TaskCategory
	 DEALLOCATE c_TaskCategory;


EXEC SP_UPDATE_QUOTE_FROM_BOM @est_ref, @userid;

END
GO
SET NOEXEC OFF;
GO

SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
-- Commit only if every procedure changed. A compile error skips one batch without
-- raising XACT_ABORT, so this count is the real check.
DECLARE @ok int = 0;
IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
BEGIN
    SELECT @ok = COUNT(*) FROM (VALUES (N'dbo.SP_CREATE_NEW_CUSTOMER'), (N'dbo.uspInsertNewCustomerDetails'), (N'dbo.usp_QMS_InitialDataLoad'), (N'dbo.usp_QMS_InitialDataLoad_CRM'), (N'dbo.CDMS_ACT_uspAddAddress'), (N'dbo.CDMS_ACT_uspAddContact'), (N'dbo.PLA_addEnquiry'), (N'dbo.PLA_addEnquiry_V2'), (N'dbo.PRC_ACT__Add_Update_BulkCTKCorroAddress'), (N'dbo.PRC_ACT_addCTKAddress_Add_Edit'), (N'dbo.PRC_ACT_addCorroAddress_Add_Edit'), (N'dbo.PRC_ACT_add_update_BulkCTKAddress'), (N'dbo.PRC_ACT_editContactDetails'), (N'dbo.PRC_ACT_uspAddAddressAdd_Edit'), (N'dbo.PRC_ACT_uspAddAddressAdd_Edit_STK'), (N'dbo.SP_ADD_CUST_ADDRESS'), (N'dbo.SP_ADD_CUST_CONTACT'), (N'dbo.SP_ADD_ENQUIRY_V1'), (N'dbo.SP_ADD_ENQUIRY_V2'), (N'dbo.SP_ADD_SITE_ADDRESS'), (N'dbo.SP_UPDATE_CUST_DTLS_BY_CUSTREF'), (N'dbo.sp_addEnquiry'), (N'dbo.usp_CloneComplexTask'), (N'dbo.usp_QMS_FinalDataLoad'), (N'dbo.usp_QMS_FinalDataLoad_CRM_MajorWork'), (N'dbo.usp_QMS_FinalDataLoad_CRM_SmallWork'), (N'dbo.usp_IncomingFromPowerConnect'), (N'dbo.sp_ACT_PLABulkBillingQuery_UPDATE'), (N'dbo.sp_ACT_addHistory'), (N'dbo.usp_CreateMPANRequestFile'), (N'dbo.usp_addHistory'), (N'dbo.usp_addHistory_CTK'), (N'dbo.SP_ADD_STANDARDS'), (N'dbo.SP_ADD_STANDARDS_CTK'), (N'dbo.SP_ApplyStandadrCode'), (N'dbo.SP_CreateStandardsFromLetters'), (N'dbo.sp_addEnquiryStandardbyAEID'), (N'dbo.CDMS_ACT_uspcust'), (N'dbo.SP_ADD_QUOTE'), (N'dbo.SP_InsertNewQuote'), (N'dbo.SP_ADD_ESTIMATE'), (N'dbo.SP_ADD_BOM'), (N'dbo.SP_ADD_BOM_FQ'), (N'dbo.SP_ADD_BOM_QMS'), (N'dbo.SP_UPDATE_BOM'), (N'dbo.usp_AddBoMWithNewPriceBook'), (N'dbo.SP_UPDATE_BOMPRICEBOOK'), (N'dbo.SP_INSERT_GETBOM'), (N'dbo.USP_INSERT_GETBOM_FQUO')) v(n)
    WHERE OBJECT_DEFINITION(OBJECT_ID(v.n)) LIKE N'%QPID_OPT V4-10%';
    IF @ok = 49
    BEGIN
        DECLARE @sql nvarchar(400), @next bigint;
        -- The tables cannot have moved on unless a writer was missed; catch up anyway.
        SELECT @next = ISNULL(MAX(CUST_ID), 0) + 1 FROM dbo.BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER WITH (READUNCOMMITTED);
        SET @sql = N'ALTER SEQUENCE dbo.SEQ_BT_CUSTOMER_MASTER RESTART WITH ' + CONVERT(nvarchar(20), @next) + N';';
        EXEC (@sql);
        SELECT @next = ISNULL(MAX(ADDRESS_ID), 0) + 1 FROM dbo.BT_ADDRESS_MASTER WITH (READUNCOMMITTED);
        SET @sql = N'ALTER SEQUENCE dbo.SEQ_BT_ADDRESS_MASTER RESTART WITH ' + CONVERT(nvarchar(20), @next) + N';';
        EXEC (@sql);
        SELECT @next = ISNULL(MAX(con_contactID), 0) + 1 FROM dbo.BT_CONTACTS_MASTER WITH (READUNCOMMITTED);
        SET @sql = N'ALTER SEQUENCE dbo.SEQ_BT_CONTACTS_MASTER RESTART WITH ' + CONVERT(nvarchar(20), @next) + N';';
        EXEC (@sql);
        SELECT @next = ISNULL(MAX(AE_AEID), 0) + 1 FROM dbo.BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER WITH (READUNCOMMITTED);
        SET @sql = N'ALTER SEQUENCE dbo.SEQ_BT_ENQUIRY_MASTER RESTART WITH ' + CONVERT(nvarchar(20), @next) + N';';
        EXEC (@sql);
        SELECT @next = ISNULL(MAX(HIST_HISTID), 0) + 1 FROM dbo.BT_ACT_HIST WITH (READUNCOMMITTED);
        SET @sql = N'ALTER SEQUENCE dbo.SEQ_BT_ACT_HIST RESTART WITH ' + CONVERT(nvarchar(20), @next) + N';';
        EXEC (@sql);
        SELECT @next = ISNULL(MAX(STA_ID), 0) + 1 FROM dbo.BT_ACT_STANDARDS WITH (READUNCOMMITTED);
        SET @sql = N'ALTER SEQUENCE dbo.SEQ_BT_ACT_STANDARDS RESTART WITH ' + CONVERT(nvarchar(20), @next) + N';';
        EXEC (@sql);
        SELECT @next = ISNULL(MAX(AE_AEID), 0) + 1 FROM dbo.BT_QUOTE_MASTER WITH (READUNCOMMITTED);
        SET @sql = N'ALTER SEQUENCE dbo.SEQ_BT_QUOTE_MASTER RESTART WITH ' + CONVERT(nvarchar(20), @next) + N';';
        EXEC (@sql);
        SELECT @next = ISNULL(MAX(AE_AEID), 0) + 1 FROM dbo.BT_ESTIMATE_MASTER WITH (READUNCOMMITTED);
        SET @sql = N'ALTER SEQUENCE dbo.SEQ_BT_ESTIMATE_MASTER RESTART WITH ' + CONVERT(nvarchar(20), @next) + N';';
        EXEC (@sql);
        SELECT @next = ISNULL(MAX(EX_ID), 0) + 1 FROM dbo.CDMS_ACT_AE_EXTRA WITH (READUNCOMMITTED);
        SET @sql = N'ALTER SEQUENCE dbo.SEQ_CDMS_ACT_AE_EXTRA RESTART WITH ' + CONVERT(nvarchar(20), @next) + N';';
        EXEC (@sql);
        SELECT @next = ISNULL(MAX(BOM_bomID), 0) + 1 FROM dbo.BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS WITH (READUNCOMMITTED);
        SET @sql = N'ALTER SEQUENCE dbo.SEQ_BT_ACT_BILLOFMATERIALS RESTART WITH ' + CONVERT(nvarchar(20), @next) + N';';
        EXEC (@sql);
        SELECT @next = ISNULL(MAX(TC_TCID), 0) + 1 FROM dbo.BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY WITH (READUNCOMMITTED);
        SET @sql = N'ALTER SEQUENCE dbo.SEQ_BT_ACT_TASKCATEGORY RESTART WITH ' + CONVERT(nvarchar(20), @next) + N';';
        EXEC (@sql);
        COMMIT TRANSACTION;
        PRINT 'DEPLOYED: V4-10 is live on all 49 procedures. Run V4-10.2 now.';
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;
        RAISERROR('NOT DEPLOYED: only %d of 49 procedures changed. Everything was rolled back. See the errors above.', 16, 1, @ok);
    END
END
ELSE
    IF OBJECT_DEFINITION(OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.SP_CREATE_NEW_CUSTOMER')) NOT LIKE N'%QPID_OPT V4-10%'
        RAISERROR('NOT DEPLOYED: nothing changed. See the errors above.', 16, 1);
GO
SET XACT_ABORT OFF;
GO
